# Local candle coverage check Г?" 2026-10-09

# FIX-156 applied-migration verification Г?" 2026-10-16

Read-only Flyway evidence (`DBQ-FIX156-V110-CHECK-CODEX`) showed V110
`fix 156 replay owned tables` already applied successfully. An in-progress
attempt to extend V110 was immediately reverted before validation/deployment;
V110 is retained in its original applied form. All additional Replay-owned
schema is placed in new forward-only V111. This is a migration-safety finding,
not a Production behavior change.

- Scope: read-only check of all 21 enabled symbols in `crypto_ai.candle`, over the two-hour window ending at the database UTC clock.
- Current state: all 63 enabled symbol/interval lanes (1m, 5m, 1h) have closed-candle records and current live ingestion. The newest 1m candle was 54 seconds old at the check.
- Continuity: 5m and 1h had no internal gaps in the inspected window. Twelve symbols had one missing 1m candle: the interval from `2026-10-08T22:18:00Z` to `2026-10-08T22:19:00Z` (KSA `01:18` to `01:19`). This coincides with the local application startup period; the check establishes the gap but does not prove its cause.
- Impact: current live signals are being generated, but the missing historical 1m candles should be reconciled before relying on uninterrupted historical indicator lineage for that startup window. No database data or code was changed by this check.

# Signal-staleness root cause Г?" 2026-10-09

- System Health CRITICAL signal-staleness alerts were confirmed as accurate for 1m and 5m analysis, not a dashboard timezone issue. At the check, candles continued through `2026-10-09T06:43:00Z` (1m) and `06:35:00Z` (5m), while the newest live signals were `04:34:03Z` (1m) and `04:30:07Z` (5m).
- The most recent 210-candle data-quality window has a synchronized gap for every enabled symbol: missing 1m open `2026-10-09T04:34:00Z` (KSA 07:34) and missing 5m open `2026-10-09T04:30:00Z` (KSA 07:30).
- Cause confirmed from the WebSocket lifecycle log: generation 1 was revoked at `2026-10-09T04:35:00.133951100Z`; generation 2 was admitted at `04:35:01.369103400Z` (1,236 ms interruption). The affected 1m and 5m candles had both closed at the reconnect boundary. Binance did not replay their final closed-kline events, leaving the already-created local rows open rather than finalized.
- `CandleDataQualityService` is configured with `minimum-candles: 210` and `maximum-missing-candles: 0`. Therefore one missing candle makes the entire relevant analysis window invalid. Runtime logs confirm `DATA_QUALITY_BLOCKED` with `Missing 1 candle(s) in the analysis window` for current 1m and 5m worker attempts.
- 1h has no comparable gap and produced a live signal at `2026-10-09T06:00:03Z` (KSA 09:00).
- No repair or configuration change was made. Correct remediation must restore authoritative missing candles rather than relaxing the zero-missing data-quality safety rule.

## Bounded local repair completed

- Authorized local repair finalized the 42 interrupted `RUNTIME` rows using Binance REST candles for the exact identities above: 21 x 1m and 21 x 5m.
- Each response was validated for the expected open and close timestamp before one transaction finalized only a row where `closed=0`. Existing closed candles, provenance, and close-transition tokens were not changed; no close-dispatch, analysis, signal, or trade was initiated by the repair.
- Verification: all 1m/5m 210-candle quality windows had zero gaps after the repair. The next live close produced new LIVE signals at `2026-10-09T07:30:07Z` for both 1m and 5m.

# ETHUSDT paper-trade 59 investigation Г?" 2026-10-09

- Scope: local `crypto_ai` paper trade `wallet_trade.id=59` for `ETHUSDT`; all database reads were bounded and read-only. Stored UTC timestamps are shown below with their KSA (`UTC+03:00`) interpretation.
- Entry: signal `trade_signal.id=69226` was generated at `2026-10-09T07:33:00.233691Z` (10:33:00.234 KSA) from the 1m candle opened at 10:32 KSA. It was a `BUY`, score 79, confidence 70, entry allowed, at 2502.77. The trade executed at `07:33:01.654548Z` (10:33:01.655 KSA), 1.42 seconds after the signal; this is not an execution-latency issue.
- Exit: `wallet_trade.id=60` closed the position at 2504.30 at `07:34:34.196100Z` (10:34:34.196 KSA) with `PROFIT_LOCK_EXIT`. The managed position recorded a high of 2505.80 and an activated profit-lock price of 2504.429502243252. The realized paper gain was about 0.02445 USDT before fees (0.015982291620 ETH x 1.53 USDT).
- Market path: ETH rose from 2485.88 at 07:54 KSA to 2502.77 at entry (+16.89, +0.68%). It reached 2505.56 at 10:25 KSA before the entry; entry was 2.79 (0.11%) below that high. The post-entry high was 2505.93 at 10:34 KSA, only 3.16 (0.13%) above entry, then the candle retraced. This supports the user's observation that entry occurred late in the observed move.
- Primary cause: the local candle-quality interruption stopped 1m ETH signals after 07:34 KSA. There were no ETH 1m signals from 07:34 to 10:26 KSA, the period containing most of the rise. The interrupted 1m and 5m rows were repaired at about 10:28 KSA, so analysis resumed only near the later consolidation/breakout attempt. This is a confirmed data-availability gap, not proof that the strategy would have bought near 07:54 KSA.
- Immediate signal sequence after recovery: at 10:30 KSA the 5m signal was a BUY but blocked by `RANGE_ENTRY_LOCATION`; at 10:31 KSA the 1m raw BUY was vetoed by a bearish BTC context; at 10:33 KSA BTC context became neutral and the 1m trend-following BUY was allowed. The entry signal itself explicitly recorded mixed higher-timeframe context (5m BUY, 1h NEUTRAL) and a weakening but ultra-close ask wall before its target.
- Assessment: the trade followed the implemented rules once local analysis resumed, and the trade was entered/exited promptly. It was nevertheless a late entry relative to the full 07:54Г?"10:33 KSA advance because the system lacked signal coverage during the main move. The data gap is an operational defect already covered by the local-candle recovery deployment gate. Whether the unobserved earlier period would have produced an executable BUY is uncertain without a controlled Replay run; none was started because that would create replay state.
# SHIBUSDT signal #89469 / position #35 Г?" 2026-10-10

- Scope: read-only local `crypto_ai` investigation of wallet trades `106`/`107`,
  paper position `35`, managed position `64`, and signal `89469`.
- Timestamp correction (2026-10-10): the preceding interpretation was wrong
  because the read-only investigation connection used MySQL's `SYSTEM` timezone
  (KSA), while the application connection explicitly sets its session to UTC.
  For position `35`, MySQL's KSA session renders `opened_at` as
  `2026-10-09 23:45:06.682175`; converting that instant to UTC yields
  `2026-10-09 20:45:06.682175`. The Inspector's main trade card parses backend
  timestamps as UTC and renders them in the browser-local timezone. Therefore,
  with a KSA browser, its displayed 23:45 entry time is correct. Do not add
  three hours to the value shown by this KSA-session SQL connection.
- Signal confirmation: `trade_signal.id=89469` was generated at
  `2026-10-09 23:45:03.498678` KSA (`20:45:03.498678` UTC), from the 1m
  candle opened at 23:44 KSA. It is `WATCH`, score 60, confidence 74,
  `WEAK_UPTREND`, `TREND_FOLLOWING`, and `final_entry_allowed=1`.
- Entry: `0.000005410`; stop `0.000005382950`; TP `0.000005433730`; the
  25%-size setup-wakeup route entered from a 5m BUY with 1h NEUTRAL context.
- Exit: highest recorded price before close was `0.000005430`, or 84.2815% of
  the original TP distance. The dynamic lock activated at `0.000005421865` and
  live price `0.000005420` breached it. At the breach the current 1m and 5m
  were WATCH and 1h was NEUTRAL. The shared exit policy therefore returned
  `PROFIT_LOCK_EXIT`; it requires bullish 1h plus supportive 5m, or WATCH 1h
  plus supportive 5m, to hold a breached lock.
- TP extension: not evaluated. Extension begins only once price is at or above
  the original TP. The recorded high remained below TP at the time of exit.
- Subsequent action: later local candles reached `0.000005500` at about 00:40
  KSA, above the original TP. This is hindsight, not evidence available at the
  exit. Local SHIB 1m/5m coverage ends at about 00:56 KSA, so any claimed
  continuation through 12:15 KSA is not verifiable from local candle evidence.
- Assessment: behavior followed the existing protection rule, without a
  confirmed execution or latency defect. A future exit-policy change could
  explicitly allow a near-TP WATCH/WATCH/NEUTRAL continuation hold under
  measurable momentum/structure conditions and a tighter safety floor; it
  requires a separate design and Replay validation.

- Direct evidence: `CandleClosedAnalysisWorker` logs at 01:28 KSA show live
  close events being processed but ending in `DATA_QUALITY_BLOCKED`, e.g.
  `warnings=[Missing 1 candle(s) in the analysis window]` for ONG/ENA/SOL and
  `Missing 3 candle(s)` for AERO. The worker correctly does not persist a new
  `trade_signal` when that zero-missing-candle gate fails.
- Assessment: this is an existing local-candle continuity/data-quality issue;
  FIX-154 higher-timeframe BUY assessment is not reached for these blocked
  1m analyses and is not the demonstrated cause of the critical health state.

# SHIBUSDT #89469 near-TP continuation investigation Г?" 2026-10-10

- Status: investigation approved; implementation is explicitly not approved.
  This workstream must not alter or delay FIX-153 release work.
- Hypothesis to test: a breached profit lock close to target may sometimes
  continue when current market structure and momentum remain supportive.
- Important counter-hypothesis: `1h=NEUTRAL` is not positive continuation
  evidence merely because 1m/5m are `WATCH`. Relaxing that guard on distance
  to TP alone could create false holds in weak or sideways markets.
- Required evidence before a policy change: use historical Replay/production
  evidence to compare the current exit rule against a hypothetical bounded
  hold using only information available at the breach. Segment results by
  1h/5m context, regime, trend structure, momentum, retracement depth and
  target-progress band. Evaluate TP hit rate, retained profit, adverse move,
  drawdown, and false-hold frequency. No post-exit price may inform entry or
  exit eligibility.
- Rejected approach: do not authorize continuation from `WATCH/WATCH/NEUTRAL`
  or near-TP distance alone.
- Code-review refinement: `PositionExitPolicy` creates a sharp tier boundary:
  with supportive 5m/current evidence, 1h `WATCH` can hold a breached lock but
  1h `NEUTRAL` falls through to `PROFIT_LOCK_EXIT`. Existing FIX-11T near-TP
  protection arms at 90% target progress and is evaluated after the ordinary
  lock-breach exit, so it could not affect #89469 at 84.28% progress.
- Fee-model refinement: `WalletAutoExecutionService` writes `fee_usdt=0` for
  paper BUY, SELL, and near-TP partial-harvest executions. Any policy Replay
  comparison must add an explicit fee/slippage model before claiming protected
  gross profit is a net gain.
- Approved experiment protocol from independent review: Production policy is
  unchanged. Compare (A) existing immediate lock exit, (B) a one-window,
  near-TP continuation using existing time-safe structure/momentum/context
  calculations, and (C) a volatility-adjusted tighter trailing exit. Use a
  pre-registered 85% TP-progress primary threshold; #89469 at 84.28% does not
  qualify and must not drive threshold selection. Require non-bearish 5m/1h,
  a fee-adjusted protected floor with modeled adverse fills, explicit numeric
  momentum/retracement definitions, no timer resets or repeated extensions,
  and strictly as-of-breach evidence. Evaluate net expectancy, drawdown,
  false-hold/adverse-move rate, and include later-decline counterexamples;
  reserve an out-of-sample period before any implementation decision.
- Final protocol controls: calculate TP progress only from immutable original
  entry and original TP; exclude and report zero/negative/invalid target
  distance. Every A/B/C candidate must use identical historical data,
  evaluation periods, and fee/slippage assumptions. Candidate B must model an
  intrawindow price path sufficiently to determine which of its emergency
  floor or target was reached first; candle high/low alone is not evidence of
  that order. Pre-register the minimum sample size, required net-expectancy
  improvement, and maximum allowed drawdown deterioration before results are
  viewed, then report the qualification count for the B cohort.
- Final cohort and comparison definition: Candidate B is eligible only in the
  85% to strictly-less-than-90% original-TP-progress band, with fresh,
  quality-valid 1h `NEUTRAL`, supportive fresh/quality-valid 5m, and required
  current-frame structure/momentum evidence. Missing, stale, invalid, or
  unknown 1h/5m evidence makes the case ineligible rather than non-bearish.
  This avoids changing or competing with existing FIX-11T behaviour at 90%.
  Pre-count the eligible cohort; insufficient sample means keep collecting.
  B-versus-A is the sole primary comparison; C is secondary/exploratory and
  must have its trailing formula fixed before Replay. Verify ATR, spread, and
  tick-size availability as of breach; if historical spread is unavailable,
  use a declared fixed assumption and sensitivity analysis.
- Final breach semantics: eligibility uses `breach_tp_progress`, calculated
  from the executable price at the exact profit-lock breach, not the historic
  high-water mark. Persist/report both `max_tp_progress` and
  `breach_tp_progress`. The continuation emergency floor is distinct from the
  breached original lock; if actual executable price (including modeled
  fee/spread/slippage) already violates that floor, exit immediately. Freeze
  numerical precision, 90%-boundary precedence (FIX-11T owns 90%+), hold
  length, tolerance, floor, costs, sample threshold, and success criteria
  before inspecting results. Report confidence intervals and chronological
  out-of-sample results; a small qualifying cohort is insufficient evidence.
- Team-2 final validity controls: create and commit a numerical pre-registration
  before any outcome is viewed, recording its immutable content hash here.
  Verify available intrabar path data; where tick/price-event evidence cannot
  order TP versus emergency-floor contact, mark the case ambiguous, apply one
  conservative adverse-move-first rule consistently, and report analyses both
  with and without those cases. Compute the floor per trade as
  `max(original_lock - tolerance, fee_adjusted_breakeven)` and report when
  breakeven binds. Compare A and B as paired outcomes on the same eligible
  breach events, reporting per-event B-minus-A and the count of later declines
  avoided by A.

# Replay-versus-Production parity audit Г?" 2026-10-10

- Scope: source-level comparison of the manual Regression/Replay pipeline with
  the current paper-trading Production path. No trading code, configuration,
  database data, or Replay result was changed.
- Confirmed shared authority: Replay regenerates technical indicators through
  `TechnicalIndicatorService`, signals through `AnalysisService`, BUY authority
  through `ExecutionIntelligenceService`, BUY/SELL validation through
  `TradeExecutionValidationService`, and normal exit, TP-continuation,
  profit-lock, and near-TP policies through the corresponding shared policy
  classes. When canonical `LEGACY`/`APPLIED` price events exist, the Replay
  consumes them in timestamp order before making the same close-candle signal
  visible, matching the documented live ordering.
- Confirmed boundary: Replay is a shadow simulation, not a transactionally
  identical copy of Production. It starts with fixed capital of 10,000 USDT,
  manages one symbol/position in isolation, uses replay-local daily budget and
  position state, and writes only `*_test` tables. It therefore cannot
  reproduce an actual wallet balance, simultaneous positions, global
  `maxOpenPositions`, daily loss limit, idempotency races, database locks, or
  real execution latency.
- Confirmed historical-evidence limitation: derivatives history is not
  persisted, so historical Replay always receives `UNAVAILABLE` derivatives
  context when that feature is enabled. Historical order-book evaluation uses
  stored snapshots only and becomes `UNAVAILABLE` when no snapshot exists,
  whereas live Production samples its in-memory/live Binance source. Such runs
  are useful simulations but are not full signal-parity proof unless the
  required historical evidence exists and is reported.
- Confirmed price-evidence limitation: a Replay run labels itself
  `EXACT_PRICE_REPLAY` whenever at least one accepted price event exists. It
  does not prove complete tick coverage for every time an open position needed
  protection. Windows with no accepted events fall back to signal prices and
  are labelled `SIGNAL_PRICE_FALLBACK`; neither fallback nor partial coverage
  should be described as tick-exact reproduction.
- Test evidence: Java 21 focused tests passed for profit-lock/TP-extension
  parity, position exit/continuation, near-TP policy, and shared sizing.
  Existing Replay-specific coverage is narrow: it does not execute a complete
  Production-versus-Replay scenario across entry, add, wallet controls,
  price-event ordering, partial harvest, and exit.
- Conclusion: Replay is suitable for controlled, manual strategy regression
  and the approved FIX-155 research work only when each run carries explicit
  evidence-quality labels. It is not yet valid to claim it is fully identical
  to Production in all phases. A future parity program must add deterministic
  scenario tests and a run-level eligibility contract before using Replay as a
  release-equivalence certificate.


===== codex-docs/TRADE_INVESTIGATION_AGENT.md =====
# Trade Investigation Agent

## Role

Investigate one Crypto AI Trader trade from durable evidence. Explain what
happened, why the system entered and exited, whether it followed the intended
rules, and whether the entry/exit was supported by the information available
at that time.

Investigations are read-only. Do not change code, configuration, services,
database records, review states, Replay work, or deployment state.

## Required input

Obtain or resolve:

- Environment and application: local, test, or production.
- Database host, port, and schema.
- One trade reference: `paper_position.id`, `wallet_trade.id`, or a symbol plus
  a bounded approximate entry-time window.
- Optional question or suspected issue.

Before analysis, confirm the symbol, trade/position ID, running version or FIX
level, and ingestion mode. Read the actual schema before choosing tables or
columns. Do not assume a table is in `crypto_ai` when it may belong to
`crypto_ai_v2`.

## Evidence rules

- Use configured database and SSH/log connections without displaying secrets.
- Use bounded, read-only queries and targeted log searches.
- Treat stored timestamps as UTC; display both UTC and KSA (`UTC+03:00`).
- Attach record IDs and timestamped log evidence to every factual conclusion.
- Distinguish **confirmed fact**, **supported inference**, and **missing or
  conflicting evidence**.
- Missing logs or records do not prove that no execution/effect occurred.
- A saved signal does not prove execution. A callback return does not prove a
  transaction committed. Under FIX-153, `market_price_event.APPLIED` proves a
  committed successful protection callback, but can be a no-op when there was
  no open position; find the position/trade mutation separately.
- Current configuration does not prove which settings were active at entry. If
  no configuration history exists, report that limitation.

## Investigation workflow

### 1. Resolve identity and environment

Link the trade reference to, where available:

- `paper_position`
- `wallet_trade`
- entry and exit `trade_signal`
- `signal_processing_work`
- wallet/position execution records

Record entry and exit times in UTC and KSA. Confirm whether the trader ran in
local-candle OFF mode or a legacy shared-market mode.

### 2. Collect a small evidence bundle

Start with exact IDs and a narrow time window. Expand only when the evidence
requires it. Collect:

| Evidence | Establishes |
| --- | --- |
| `paper_position` | lifecycle, entry/exit, stop, target, status |
| `wallet_trade` | executed quantity, prices, fees, linked signal |
| `trade_signal` | decision, strategy, scores, entry eligibility, reasons |
| `signal_processing_work` | claim, attempt, completion, expiry, uncertainty |
| `candle` / `technical_indicator` | market data and analysis inputs available then |
| `market_price_event` | intra-candle protection observations and outcomes |
| configuration/version evidence | applicable rules, when historically available |
| application and timing logs | analysis, execution, protection, exceptions, waits |

Avoid fetching large signal explanation fields until they are needed.

### 3. Reconstruct the timeline

Build a timeline, explicitly showing missing stages:

`candle closed Г+' signal saved Г+' work claimed Г+' locks acquired Г+' entry committed Г+' protection observations Г+' exit decision Г+' exit committed`

Measure separately:

- candle close to signal generation;
- signal generation to processing/entry;
- observation time to protection completion;
- exit-trigger evidence to recorded exit.

Correlate delays only with logs/events for the same symbol, position, signal,
or price-event identity. A nearby slow SQL query is not proof of causation.

### 4. Assess entry correctness and quality

Use only evidence that was available at entry. Check:

- signal mode, closed-candle identity, freshness, and data quality;
- final decision and entry-allowed result;
- BTC context, higher-timeframe, ATR, liquidity, risk/reward, sizing, and
  strategy gates that applied at that time;
- correct signal/position linkage and absence of duplicate/stale-retry entry;
- quantity, wallet balance, risk limits, fees, and fill/reference-price gap.

Return two separate conclusions:

- **Execution correctness:** followed or violated the implemented rules.
- **Entry quality:** supported, weak, or unverifiable from contemporaneous
  evidence.

Subsequent price movement may evaluate the outcome; it must never be presented
as information the entry decision had.

### 5. Reconstruct protection and stop-loss behavior

For FIX-153, inspect accepted `LEGACY`/`APPLIED` price events and retain
`PENDING`, `NON_CANONICAL`, and `PROTECTION_NOT_APPLIED` records as evidence
gaps or exclusions. Never substitute candle OHLC for absent intra-candle price
evidence.

For each relevant accepted observation:

1. Determine the then-active stop, target, trailing stop, and profit-lock state.
2. Find the first accepted observation that met an exit threshold.
3. Confirm that protection evaluated it and identify the resulting position or
   wallet execution record.
4. Explain delays, rejections, stale/out-of-order classification, exceptions,
   lock waits, or pool pressure.

Classify the exit path accurately: fixed stop-loss, trailing stop, profit-lock,
take-profit, signal-driven exit, manual exit, or unknown.

### 6. Assess exit correctness and quality

Compare the recorded exit reason, quantity, and price with trigger evidence.
Check threshold reach, accepted-observation identity, protection timing, and
the committed execution. Calculate maximum favourable/adverse movement during
the holding period separately from the contemporaneous exit assessment.

### 7. Reconcile financial results

Recalculate using execution records:

- bought/sold quantities and weighted entry/exit prices;
- gross P&L, fees, and net P&L;
- partial exits, remaining quantity, and realized versus unrealized values.

Flag discrepancies among wallet records, position records, and UI output.

### 8. Diagnose cause and use Replay carefully

Classify findings as one or more of:

- strategy decision;
- data freshness or coverage;
- analysis delay;
- execution failure;
- protection failure;
- database, lock, or connection-pool contention;
- accounting/UI discrepancy;
- insufficient evidence.

Run Replay only as a controlled comparison with equivalent candles, accepted
prices, strategy version, and settings. State every unavailable input or
material difference. Never claim parity by substituting candle OHLC for missing
price evidence or treating `PENDING` as accepted.

## Required final report

1. **Trade identity and environment** Г?" IDs, symbol, version/mode, scope.
2. **UTC/KSA timeline** Г?" evidence-backed sequence and latency breakdown.
3. **Entry** Г?" execution correctness and entry quality.
4. **Exit** Г?" execution correctness and exit quality.
5. **Stop-loss/profit-lock** Г?" worked, failed, or unverifiable, with evidence.
6. **P&L reconciliation** Г?" quantities, prices, fees, and discrepancies.
7. **Root cause and confidence** Г?" confirmed defect, valid loss, configuration
   issue, delay, or insufficient evidence.
8. **Evidence references** Г?" record IDs, query/log timestamps, code/config
   references.
9. **Missing evidence** Г?" exactly what prevents a stronger conclusion.
10. **Recommended correction and regression checks** Г?" only when justified.

A profitable trade can violate the rules, and a losing trade can be a correct
execution. State those independently.


===== codex-docs/TRADING_RULES.md =====
# FIX-154 higher-timeframe BUY rule Г?" 2026-10-10

- `data_quality_valid=true` is required before a bearish 1h signal can be
  treated as trusted. `NULL` is unknown and fails closed for bearish evidence.
- A trusted `STRONG_SELL` at `confidence_score >= 70` blocks the BUY.
- A trusted `SELL`, or trusted `STRONG_SELL` below 70, is a SOFT conflict:
  profiles receive effective 1h `NEUTRAL`, and any allowed initial BUY is
  capped at 25%.
- A bearish 5m still returns `5M_BEARISH_VETO` before any 1h result.
- Existing progressive/add rules retain their raw 1h requirement; a SOFT 1h
  never authorizes an add.

# FIX-153 candle continuity rule Г?" 2026-10-10

- Missing price evidence never becomes invented OHLCV data and never bypasses
  the data-quality gate.
- Automatic reconciliation may restore only one exact, final Binance candle.
  Its recovered/backfilled row has no close token and cannot directly cause
  analysis, a signal, or a trade.
- A later normal live close is the only route that resumes ordinary signal
  generation after a repaired quality window.

===== agent-docs/runs/fts-WINDOW2-20261025-180002/private/CODEX/iteration03-codex.txt =====
## Iteration 3 Г?" Log and timing forensics

### Findings

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** No database-result artifacts exist in the permitted `db/CODEX/` folder. No trade-specific timing, price-event, exit-audit, or log correlation is therefore available for frozen positions `182, 172, 162, 158, 143, 137`.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The active application log contains only startup activity: `2026-10-19 14:32:18.902 UTC` (`17:32:18.902 KSA`) through `2026-10-19 14:32:36.235 UTC` (`17:32:36.235 KSA`). It contains zero matching FIX-129, FIX-131, FIX-137, FIX-153, FIX-127, or live-exit markers. This is not evidence about any frozen trade.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Normal fast kline pipeline stages are intentionally not timing-logged. `KlineTransactionCoordinator` invokes `KlineTiming.measure(..., false, ...)` for input, protection, observer, and close-dispatch. `KlineTiming` emits a timing record only for failure or elapsed time at least 1,000 ms. Therefore absence of a FIX-129 timing row cannot establish that a normal sub-second stage did not run.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Timing diagnostics are explicitly best-effort, not a complete ledger. `KlineTimingStore` uses a bounded asynchronous queue and can report `PERSIST_DROPPED`; shutdown can lose queued rows. Application-file logging is also asynchronous with `neverBlock=true`; a missing archived file event cannot prove the underlying event did not occur.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** There is useful reconstructable evidence when records exist:
  - FIX-131 closed-candle logs carry receipt correlation, Binance event time, receipt time, input-commit time, and receipt-to-commit duration.
  - FIX-137 worker logs carry event/symbol/interval/candle identity, stage and elapsed duration.
  - FIX-153 signal-save logs carry candle close, signal generation, row-creation, service-return times, and close-to-generation/insertion delays.
  - `signal_processing_work` provides claim/attempt/completion/expiry state.
  - `market_price_event` provides observed price time and durable protection outcome.
  - `production_exit_audit` links the paper position to the terminal trigger and context signal.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The primary live-exit logs omit a paper-position ID, wallet-trade ID, market-price-event ID, observation time, and kline-receipt correlation. `paper_position.closed_at`, wallet trade `executed_at`, and exit audit `audited_at` are application `Instant.now()` values written before transaction completion; they are useful event timestamps but do not prove database commit time.

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** No delay, stop-loss failure, stale observation, late execution, or logging defect can be attributed to any frozen trade without the exact DB lifecycle records and a time/symbol map to search archived logs.

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** The supplied snapshot metadata remains internally inconsistent: `2026-10-25 15:00:04 UTC` converts to `2026-10-25 18:00:04 KSA`, while the supplied snapshot KSA is `2026-10-25 18:00:02`. This two-second discrepancy does not identify a trade defect.

```sql
WHERE paper_position_id IN (182, 172, 162, 158, 143, 137)
ORDER BY paper_position_id, audited_at;

SELECT *
FROM crypto_ai.wallet_managed_position
WHERE entry_signal_id IN (
  SELECT signal_id
  FROM crypto_ai.paper_position
  WHERE id IN (182, 172, 162, 158, 143, 137)
)
ORDER BY opened_at;

SELECT *
FROM crypto_ai.wallet_trade
WHERE signal_id IN (
  SELECT signal_id
  FROM crypto_ai.paper_position
  WHERE id IN (182, 172, 162, 158, 143, 137)
)
OR signal_id IN (
  SELECT exit_signal_id
  FROM crypto_ai.paper_position
  WHERE id IN (182, 172, 162, 158, 143, 137)
)
ORDER BY executed_at;
```

### DB_REQUEST

QueryId: DBQ-CODEX-I3-004  
Purpose: Reconstruct protection observations and available slow/failure pipeline timing during each frozen positionГ?Ts holding interval.  
SQL:
```sql
WITH frozen AS (
  SELECT id, symbol, opened_at, closed_at
  FROM crypto_ai.paper_position
  WHERE id IN (182, 172, 162, 158, 143, 137)
)
SELECT
  f.id AS paper_position_id,
  mpe.*
FROM frozen f
JOIN crypto_ai.market_price_event mpe
  ON mpe.symbol = f.symbol
 AND mpe.observed_at >= f.opened_at
 AND mpe.observed_at <= f.closed_at
ORDER BY f.id, mpe.observed_at, mpe.id;

WITH frozen AS (
  SELECT id, symbol, opened_at, closed_at
  FROM crypto_ai.paper_position
  WHERE id IN (182, 172, 162, 158, 143, 137)
)
SELECT
  f.id AS paper_position_id,
  st.*
FROM frozen f
JOIN crypto_ai.fix129_stage_timing st
  ON st.symbol = f.symbol
 AND st.started_at >= f.opened_at
 AND st.started_at <= f.closed_at
ORDER BY f.id, st.started_at, st.id;
```
### END_DB_REQUEST

## FINDINGS

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** No permitted DB-result artifacts exist in `db/CODEX`; the six frozen positions therefore cannot yet be assigned an entry, exit route, or defect conclusion.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Current source has two materially different exit routes:
  - Live-price protection: `market_price_event` Г+' `LivePositionProtectionService` Г+' wallet mechanical exit Г+' `paper_position` close Г+' `production_exit_audit`.
  - Signal processing: durable `signal_processing_work` Г+' `PaperTradingService` Г+' paper close plus signal-linked wallet exit/audit.
  
  `paper_position.close_reason` alone is insufficient to distinguish the actual path; `production_exit_audit`, wallet records, and price-event evidence are required.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Entry order is wallet-first. `PaperTradingService` obtains a fresh canonical price, revalidates the BUY, writes the wallet BUY, then creates `paper_position` using that fresh execution price and executed quantity. The entry signalГ?Ts `latest_price` is decision evidence, not necessarily the fill price.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** For live protection, TP and SL are checked before profit-lock, normal confirmed exits, and near-TP handling. Mechanical exits are wallet-first and use the observed live price. A price event is marked `APPLIED` only in the effects transaction after the protection callback completes; `APPLIED` can still be a no-position/no-op callback.

- **AGENT INFERENCE Г?" candidate temporal-integrity issue, not trade-specific proof:** `LivePositionProtectionService.onPrice(symbol, price, observedAt, eventId)` validates the eventГ?Ts `observedAt`, but subsequently reads the newest 1m/5m/1h signals without constraining `generated_at <= observedAt`. Thus, if protection processing is delayed but still within the 15-second price-age limit, a profit-lock or signal-driven exit may use a signal generated after the actual price observation. Near-TP logic has a limited Г?onot future relative to evaluation timeГ?? guard, but not an observation-time guard. This requires DB evidence before attributing it to any frozen trade.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Paper and wallet P&L are gross-price calculations in the reviewed routes; paper close computes `(exit - entry) A- quantity`, while automatic wallet trades persist `fee_usdt = 0`. The six losses are valid as negative paper P&L by the frozen selection, but their fee-adjusted economic loss cannot be inferred from current source.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The supplied snapshot values remain inconsistent: `2026-10-25 15:00:04 UTC` converts to `2026-10-25 18:00:04 KSA`, while the supplied KSA snapshot is `18:00:02`. This has no demonstrated impact on a frozen trade.

## EVIDENCE

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** `PaperTradingService.java:106-417` Г?" signal route, fresh-price entry, wallet-first BUY, and paper-position creation.
- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** `LivePositionProtectionService.java:53-247, 391-411` Г?" price-event validation; TP/SL/profit-lock/normal-exit order; mechanical wallet-first exit; paper close and audit.
- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** `PositionExitPolicy.java:18-94` Г?" confirmed 1m/5m and 1h exit conditions; profit-lock hold/exit logic.
- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** `WalletAutoExecutionService.java:79-197, 475-565` Г?" execution-time BUY price persistence; gross paper-wallet fills; mechanical exit ordering.
- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** `MarketPriceEventService.java:40-115` Г?" stale/future rejection, ordering, and durable protection outcome semantics.
- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** `ProductionExitAuditService.java:27-59` Г?" immutable close-trigger/context evidence.
- **EXTERNAL RESEARCH:** None.

## UNKNOWNS

- For IDs `182, 172, 162, 158, 143, 137`: symbol, entry/exit UTC timestamps and KSA conversions, prices, quantity, P&L, close trigger, signal context, stop/TP geometry, and whether protection observed the threshold promptly.
- Whether the candidate observation-time/signal-time issue occurred for any frozen trade.
- Whether the current workspace source/version and configuration were active when these positions closed.
- Wallet/position/audit linkage and whether any selected paper close lacked the corresponding durable execution evidence.

### DB_REQUEST

QueryId: DBQ-CODEX-I4-003  
Purpose: After DBQ-CODEX-I4-001 confirms the code-mapped columns, reconstruct the durable wallet, audit, signal, and live-price route for only the six frozen positions.  
SQL:
```sql
SELECT
    p.id AS paper_position_id,
    a.*
FROM crypto_ai.paper_position p
LEFT JOIN crypto_ai.production_exit_audit a
    ON a.paper_position_id = p.id
WHERE p.id IN (182, 172, 162, 158, 143, 137)
ORDER BY p.id, a.audited_at;

SELECT
    p.id AS paper_position_id,
    wmp.*
FROM crypto_ai.paper_position p
LEFT JOIN crypto_ai.wallet_managed_position wmp
    ON wmp.entry_signal_id = p.signal_id
WHERE p.id IN (182, 172, 162, 158, 143, 137)
ORDER BY p.id, wmp.opened_at;

SELECT
    p.id AS paper_position_id,
    wt.*
FROM crypto_ai.paper_position p
JOIN crypto_ai.wallet_trade wt
    ON wt.signal_id = p.signal_id
    OR wt.signal_id = p.exit_signal_id
WHERE p.id IN (182, 172, 162, 158, 143, 137)
ORDER BY p.id, wt.executed_at, wt.id;

SELECT
    p.id AS paper_position_id,
    mpe.*
FROM crypto_ai.paper_position p
JOIN crypto_ai.market_price_event mpe
    ON mpe.symbol = p.symbol
   AND mpe.observed_at >= p.opened_at
   AND mpe.observed_at <= p.closed_at
WHERE p.id IN (182, 172, 162, 158, 143, 137)
ORDER BY p.id, mpe.observed_at, mpe.id;
```
### END_DB_REQUEST

  88:     public Optional<PaperPosition> processSignal(TradeSignal signal) {
  89:         if (processingCoordinator == null) return processSignalBody(signal);
  90:         if (signal == null || signal.getId() == null) throw new IllegalArgumentException("Persisted signal required");
  91:         return processingCoordinator.process(signal.getId(), false, this::processSignalBody);
  92:     }
  93: 
  94:     @Transactional(propagation = org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Propagation.NEVER)
  95:     public Optional<PaperPosition> recoverRegisteredSignal(long signalId) {
  96:         return processingCoordinator.process(signalId, true, this::processSignalBody);
  97:     }
  98: 
  99:     /** FIX-132: explicit delivery ownership, never a natural-key inference. */
 100:     @Transactional(propagation = org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Propagation.NEVER)
 101:     public Optional<PaperPosition> processSharedSignal(TradeSignal signal, long sourceEventId) {
 102:         return processingCoordinator.process(signal.getId(),false,this::processSignalBody,sourceEventId);
 103:     }
 104: 
 105:     /** FIX-127: existing decision order; invoked inside the coordinator's transaction. */
 106:     private Optional<PaperPosition> processSignalBody(TradeSignal signal) {
 107:         if (signal == null) {
 108:             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Trade signal is required");
 109:         }
 110: 
 111:         String symbol = normalizeSymbol(signal.getSymbol());
 112: 
 113:         // FIX-127: position management persists advice and can request POSITION_STOP_LOSS.
 114:         // Keep its existing decision order, but acquire the initial managed lock before entering here.
 115:         if (positionManagementService != null) {
 116:             try {
 117:                 positionManagementService.analyze(signal);
 118:             } catch (RuntimeException ex) {
 119:                 log.warn("Position advisory failed for signal {}: {}", signal.getId(), ex.getMessage());
 120:             }
 121:         }
 122:         Optional<PaperPosition> openPosition = positionRepository
 123:                 .findBySymbolAndStatus(symbol, PositionStatus.OPEN);
 124: 
 125:         if (openPosition.isPresent()) {
 126:             PaperPosition position = openPosition.get();
 127:             BigDecimal price = signal.getLatestPrice();
 128: 
 129:             // Never treat a delayed timeframe candle close as the current mark price.
 130:             // Context from that signal is still processed below, but TP/SL/profit-lock
 131:             // mechanics require a post-entry market observation. Live production protection
 132:             // is independently driven by LivePositionProtectionService.onPrice(...).
 133:             boolean authoritativePrice = priceAuthorityPolicy.canUseSignalPrice(signal, position.getOpenedAt());
 134:             DynamicProfitLockService.Evaluation profitLock = authoritativePrice
 135:                     ? dynamicProfitLockService.evaluate(signal)
 136:                     : DynamicProfitLockService.Evaluation.inactive(
 137:                             "Signal price predates the open position and is context-only for mechanical protection.");
 138: 
 139:             // FIX-118 Production trace for the independent signal-price Profit Lock authority.
 140:             // The explicit ProfitLockState now also shows whether the historical lock is ACTIVE
 141:             // or temporarily non-executable while TP extension is rebasing.
 142:             if (authoritativePrice && (profitLock.active() || profitLock.triggered() || profitLock.rebasing())) {
 143:                 log.info("[FIX-118][PRODUCTION][SIGNAL_PATH] signalId={}, positionId={}, symbol={}, interval={}, price={}, " +
 144:                                 "active={}, progressPct={}, activationPct={}, lock={}, state={}, triggered={}",
 145:                         signal.getId(), profitLock.walletPositionId(), symbol, signal.getInterval(), price,
 146:                         profitLock.active(), profitLock.progressPercent(), profitLock.activationPercent(),
 147:                         profitLock.lockPrice(), profitLock.state(), profitLock.triggered());
 148:             }
 149: 
 150:             // FIX-067: The wallet-managed position is the authoritative Production source for
 151:             // a dynamically extended take-profit.  paper_position historically retained the
 152:             // entry-time TP, so a fresh 1m signal could close against that stale threshold even
 153:             // after LivePositionProtectionService had persisted TAKE_PROFIT_EXTENDED.
 154:             // Prefer the OPEN managed TP and repair the paper snapshot when they differ. This is
 155:             // deliberately NOT a TP-policy change: it only makes both existing exit paths compare
 156:             // against the same already-approved target.
 157:             BigDecimal authoritativeTakeProfit = position.getTakeProfit();
 158:             WalletManagedPosition managedPosition = walletManagedPositionRepository
 159:                     .findTopBySymbolAndStatusOrderByOpenedAtDesc(symbol, "OPEN")
 160:                     .orElse(null);
 161:             if (managedPosition != null && managedPosition.getTakeProfitUsdt() != null) {
 162:                 authoritativeTakeProfit = managedPosition.getTakeProfitUsdt();
 163:                 if (position.getTakeProfit() == null
 164:                         || position.getTakeProfit().compareTo(authoritativeTakeProfit) != 0) {
 165:                     position.setTakeProfit(authoritativeTakeProfit);
 166:                     positionRepository.save(position);
 167:                 }
 168:             }
 169: 
 170:             if (authoritativePrice && authoritativeTakeProfit != null
 171:                     && price.compareTo(authoritativeTakeProfit) >= 0) {
 172:                 return Optional.of(closeFromSignal(position, signal, PositionStatus.CLOSED,
 173:                         "TAKE_PROFIT", "Price reached the authoritative configured take-profit target."));
 174:             }
 175: 
 176:             if (authoritativePrice && profitLock.triggered()) {
 177:                 log.info("[FIX-118][PRODUCTION][SIGNAL_TRIGGER] signalId={}, positionId={}, symbol={}, price={}, lock={}, progressPct={}",
 178:                         signal.getId(), profitLock.walletPositionId(), symbol, price, profitLock.lockPrice(), profitLock.progressPercent());
 179:                 return Optional.of(closeFromProfitLock(position, signal, profitLock));
 180:             }
 181: 
 182:             if (authoritativePrice && price.compareTo(position.getStopLoss()) <= 0) {
 183:                 return Optional.of(closeFromSignal(position, signal, PositionStatus.STOPPED,
 184:                         "STOP_LOSS", "Price reached the configured stop loss."));
 185:             }
 186: 
 187:             // FIX-118: in TP_EXTENSION_REBASE the old Profit Lock cannot execute, but the
 188:             // direct protected-profit floor still applies. Stop Loss remains absolute and
 189:             // therefore keeps priority immediately above this branch.
 190:             if (authoritativePrice && profitLock.hardProfitFloorTriggered()) {
 191:                 log.info("[FIX-118][PRODUCTION][SIGNAL_REBASE_HARD_FLOOR] signalId={}, positionId={}, symbol={}, price={}, floor={}",
 192:                         signal.getId(), profitLock.walletPositionId(), symbol, price, profitLock.hardProfitFloor());
 193:                 return Optional.of(closeFromSignal(position, signal, PositionStatus.CLOSED,
 194:                         "PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT", profitLock.explanation()));
 195:             }
 196: 
 197:             if (signal.getDecision() == SignalDecision.SELL
 198:                     || signal.getDecision() == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL) {
 199:                 if (profitLock.active()) {
 200:                     log.info("Normal wallet SELL suppressed because Dynamic Profit Lock is active: signalId={}, symbol={}, protectedPrice={}, currentPrice={}",
 201:                             signal.getId(), signal.getSymbol(), profitLock.lockPrice(), signal.getLatestPrice());
 202:                     return Optional.of(position);
 203:                 }
 204:                 TradeExecutionValidationService.ValidationResult validation =
 205:                         executionValidationService.validateSell(signal);
 206:                 if (validation.allowed()) {
 207:                     return Optional.of(closeFromSignal(position, signal, PositionStatus.CLOSED,
 208:                             signal.getDecision().name(), signal.getExplanation()));
 209:                 }
 210:                 log.info("Normal wallet SELL rejected: signalId={}, symbol={}, interval={}, reason={}, detail={}",
 211:                         signal.getId(), signal.getSymbol(), signal.getInterval(),
 212:                         validation.code(), validation.explanation());
 213:             }
 214: 
 215:             // Progressive Position Building may add to an existing position only after
 216:             // TP / Profit Lock / SL / validated SELL logic had first priority.
 217:             if ("1m".equals(signal.getInterval()) && !profitLock.active()) {
 218:                 WalletManagedPosition managed = walletManagedPositionRepository
 219:                         .findTopBySymbolAndStatusOrderByOpenedAtDesc(symbol, "OPEN")
 220:                         .orElse(null);
 221:                 if (managed != null) {
 222:                     ExecutionIntelligenceService.ExecutionDecision addDecision =
 223:                             executionIntelligenceService.evaluateBuy(
 224:                                     signal,
 225:                                     managed.getAllocatedPositionPercent(),
 226:                                     managed.getEntryStage());
 227:                     if (addDecision.allowed()) {
 228:                         if (walletTradeRepository
 229:                                 .findTopBySignalIdAndSideAndStatusOrderByExecutedAtDesc(
 230:                                         signal.getId(), "BUY", "EXECUTED")
 231:                                 .isPresent()) {
 232:                             return Optional.of(position);
 233:                         }
 234:                         int addPercent = addDecision.positionPercent();
 235:                         // FIX-056: progressive adds use the same fresh canonical price authority
 236:                         // as initial entries. Signal.latestPrice remains decision-time evidence only.
 237:                         ExecutionPriceAuthorityService.ExecutionPrice fresh = executionPriceAuthorityService
 238:                                 .resolve(symbol, Instant.now()).orElse(null);
 239:                         if (fresh == null) {
 240:                             log.info("Progressive BUY skipped: no fresh execution price for {}", symbol);
 241:                             return Optional.of(position);
 242:                         }
 243:                         ExecutionIntelligenceService.ExecutionDecision freshDecision =
 244:                                 executionIntelligenceService.revalidateAtExecutionPrice(signal, addDecision, fresh.price());
 245:                         if (!freshDecision.allowed()) return Optional.of(position);
 246:                         addPercent = freshDecision.positionPercent();
 247:                         ExecutionIntelligenceService.EntryQuality entryQuality =
 248:                                 executionIntelligenceService.assessEntryQualityAtPrice(signal, fresh.price());
 249:                         boolean walletAdded = walletAutoExecutionService.executeBuy(
 250:                                 signal, fresh.price(), fresh.observedAt(), addPercent,
 251:                                 "Execution Intelligence [" + freshDecision.source() + "] " + freshDecision.explanation()
 252:                                         + " FreshPrice=" + fresh.price() + "@" + fresh.observedAt(),
 253:                                 freshDecision.source(),
 254:                                 entryQuality.score());
 255:                         if (walletAdded) {
 256:                             BigDecimal addedQuantity = walletTradeRepository
 257:                                     .findTopBySignalIdAndSideAndStatusOrderByExecutedAtDesc(
 258:                                             signal.getId(), "BUY", "EXECUTED")
 259:                                     .map(com.crypto.wallet.domain.WalletTrade::getQuantity)
 260:                                     .filter(q -> q != null && q.signum() > 0)
 261:                                     .orElse(BigDecimal.ZERO);
 262:                             if (addedQuantity.signum() > 0) {
 263:                                 BigDecimal oldCost = position.getEntryPrice().multiply(position.getQuantity(), MC);
 264:                                 BigDecimal addCost = fresh.price().multiply(addedQuantity, MC);
 265:                                 BigDecimal newQuantity = position.getQuantity().add(addedQuantity);
 266:                                 position.setQuantity(newQuantity);
 267:                                 position.setEntryPrice(oldCost.add(addCost, MC).divide(newQuantity, MC));
 268:                                 if (signal.getStopLoss() != null
 269:                                         && signal.getStopLoss().compareTo(position.getStopLoss()) > 0) {
 270:                                     position.setStopLoss(signal.getStopLoss());
 271:                                 }
 272:                                 if (signal.getTakeProfit() != null
 273:                                         && signal.getTakeProfit().compareTo(position.getTakeProfit()) > 0) {
 274:                                     position.setTakeProfit(signal.getTakeProfit());
 275:                                 }
 276:                                 position.setEntryReason(position.getEntryReason()
 277:                                         + " | Progressive add [" + addDecision.source() + "] "
 278:                                         + addPercent + "% at " + fresh.price()
 279:                                         + "; Entry Quality=" + entryQuality.score() + "/100.");
 280:                                 positionRepository.save(position);
 281:                             }
 282:                             executionIntelligenceService.markExecuted(signal, addDecision);
 283:                         }
 284:                     }
 285:                 }
 286:             }
 287: 
 288:             // Non-execution timeframes and non-executable decisions only update context/advisory state.
 289:             return Optional.of(position);
 290:         }
 291: 
 292:         // FIX-014: a fresh 5m BUY transition may wake an already-live deferred 1m BUY
 293:         // opportunity, but 5m never executes directly. The returned executionSignal is the
 294:         // latest fresh 1m timing/risk plan; all existing SETUP_TIMEFRAME_ATR and hard-risk
 295:         // guards remain authoritative. Normal 1m BUY handling is unchanged.
 296:         TradeSignal executionSignal = signal;
 297:         ExecutionIntelligenceService.ExecutionDecision executionDecision;
 298:         ExecutionIntelligenceService.SetupWakeupEvaluation wakeup =
 299:                 executionIntelligenceService.evaluateSetupTimeframeWakeup(signal, 0);
 300:         if (!wakeup.present()) {
 301:             // FIX-023: a fresh executable 5m BUY transition may also wake an existing
 302:             // opportunity after liquidity/confirmation improves. The returned signal is
 303:             // still the latest 1m timing/risk plan; 5m never executes independently.
 304:             wakeup = executionIntelligenceService.evaluateConfirmedSetupWakeup(signal, 0);
 305:         }
 306:         if (wakeup.present() && wakeup.decision().allowed()) {
 307:             executionSignal = wakeup.executionSignal();
 308:             executionDecision = wakeup.decision();
 309:         } else {
 310:             executionDecision = executionIntelligenceService.evaluateBuy(signal);
 311:         }
 312: 
 313:         if (!executionDecision.allowed()) {
 314:             log.info("Execution Intelligence did not open a BUY: signalId={}, symbol={}, interval={}, state={}, source={}, code={}, evidenceScore={}, buys={}, watches={}, detail={}",
 315:                     signal.getId(), signal.getSymbol(), signal.getInterval(), executionDecision.state(),
 316:                     executionDecision.source(), executionDecision.code(),
 317:                     executionDecision.evidence().evidenceScore(), executionDecision.evidence().buyCount(),
 318:                     executionDecision.evidence().watchCount(), executionDecision.explanation());
 319:             return Optional.empty();
 320:         }
 321: 
 322:         if (positionRepository.countByStatus(PositionStatus.OPEN) >= properties.maxOpenPositions()) {
 323:             return Optional.empty();
 324:         }
 325: 
 326:         enforceDailyLossLimit();
 327: 
 328:         int atrPercent = executionSignal.getAtrRecommendedPositionPercent() <= 0
 329:                 ? 100
 330:                 : executionSignal.getAtrRecommendedPositionPercent();
 331:         int executionPercent = executionDecision.positionPercent();
 332:         int effectivePositionPercent = Math.max(1,
 333:                 Math.min(100, (int) Math.round(atrPercent * executionPercent / 100.0)));
 334:         BigDecimal positionScale = BigDecimal.valueOf(effectivePositionPercent)
 335:                 .divide(BigDecimal.valueOf(100), MC);
 336:         BigDecimal riskAmount = properties.paperAccountBalance()
 337:                 .multiply(properties.riskPerTradePercent(), MC)
 338:                 .divide(BigDecimal.valueOf(100), MC)
 339:                 .multiply(positionScale, MC);
 340:         // FIX-056: resolve a canonical execution-time price AFTER all signal/HTF gates but
 341:         // BEFORE risk sizing and wallet persistence. Never size from a stale signal snapshot.
 342:         ExecutionPriceAuthorityService.ExecutionPrice freshExecutionPrice = executionPriceAuthorityService
 343:                 .resolve(symbol, Instant.now()).orElse(null);
 344:         if (freshExecutionPrice == null) {
 345:             log.info("Execution Intelligence approved signal {} but no fresh canonical execution price was available; BUY skipped.",
 346:                     executionSignal.getId());
 347:             return Optional.empty();
 348:         }
 349:         executionDecision = executionIntelligenceService.revalidateAtExecutionPrice(
 350:                 executionSignal, executionDecision, freshExecutionPrice.price());
 351:         if (!executionDecision.allowed()) {
 352:             log.info("BUY revalidation rejected signal {} at fresh price {}: {} - {}",
 353:                     executionSignal.getId(), freshExecutionPrice.price(), executionDecision.code(), executionDecision.explanation());
 354:             return Optional.empty();
 355:         }
 356:         // Recompute exposure after fresh-price Entry Quality because the final guard may
 357:         // reduce the approved allocation further than the signal-time decision did.
 358:         executionPercent = executionDecision.positionPercent();
 359:         effectivePositionPercent = Math.max(1,
 360:                 Math.min(100, (int) Math.round(atrPercent * executionPercent / 100.0)));
 361:         positionScale = BigDecimal.valueOf(effectivePositionPercent)
 362:                 .divide(BigDecimal.valueOf(100), MC);
 363:         riskAmount = properties.paperAccountBalance()
 364:                 .multiply(properties.riskPerTradePercent(), MC)
 365:                 .divide(BigDecimal.valueOf(100), MC)
 366:                 .multiply(positionScale, MC);
 367:         BigDecimal riskPerUnit = freshExecutionPrice.price().subtract(executionSignal.getStopLoss(), MC).abs();
 368: 
 369:         if (riskPerUnit.signum() == 0) {
 370:             throw new IllegalStateException("Invalid stop-loss distance");
 371:         }
 372: 
 373:         BigDecimal riskModelQuantity = riskAmount.divide(riskPerUnit, MC);
 374: 
 375:         final boolean walletExecuted;
 376:         try {
 377:             ExecutionIntelligenceService.EntryQuality entryQuality =
 378:                     executionIntelligenceService.assessEntryQualityAtPrice(executionSignal, freshExecutionPrice.price());
 379:             walletExecuted = walletAutoExecutionService.executeBuy(executionSignal, freshExecutionPrice.price(), freshExecutionPrice.observedAt(), effectivePositionPercent,
 380:                     "Execution Intelligence [" + executionDecision.source() + "] " + executionDecision.explanation()
 381:                             + " DecisionPrice=" + executionSignal.getLatestPrice()
 382:                             + ", executionPrice=" + freshExecutionPrice.price()
 383:                             + ", priceObservedAt=" + freshExecutionPrice.observedAt(),
 384:                     executionDecision.source(),
 385:                     entryQuality.score());
 386:         } catch (RuntimeException ex) {
 387:             log.error("Automatic wallet BUY failed for signal {}: {}", executionSignal.getId(), ex.getMessage(), ex);
 388:             return Optional.empty();
 389:         }
 390:         if (!walletExecuted) {
 391:             log.info("Execution Intelligence approved signal {} but wallet controls declined execution; no paper position was created.", executionSignal.getId());
 392:             return Optional.empty();
 393:         }
 394: 
 395:         BigDecimal executedQuantity = walletTradeRepository
 396:                 .findTopBySignalIdAndSideAndStatusOrderByExecutedAtDesc(executionSignal.getId(), "BUY", "EXECUTED")
 397:                 .map(com.crypto.wallet.domain.WalletTrade::getQuantity)
 398:                 .filter(q -> q != null && q.signum() > 0)
 399:                 .orElse(riskModelQuantity);
 400: 
 401:         executionIntelligenceService.markExecuted(executionSignal, executionDecision);
 402:         PaperPosition position = positionRepository.save(PaperPosition.builder()
 403:                 .symbol(symbol)
 404:                 .side(PositionSide.BUY)
 405:                 .status(PositionStatus.OPEN)
 406:                 .quantity(executedQuantity)
 407:                 .entryPrice(freshExecutionPrice.price())
 408:                 .stopLoss(executionSignal.getStopLoss())
 409:                 .takeProfit(executionSignal.getTakeProfit())
 410:                 .signal(executionSignal)
 411:                 .entryReason(executionSignal.getExplanation() + " | Execution Intelligence [" + executionDecision.source() + "]: " + executionDecision.explanation() + " Effective position " + effectivePositionPercent + "%.")
 412:                 .openedAt(Instant.now())
 413:                 .build());
 414:         return Optional.of(position);
 415:     }

  62:     public ValidationResult validateBuy(TradeSignal executionSignal, int entryQualityScore) {
  63:         ValidationResult structural = validateBaseSignal(executionSignal, true);
  64:         if (!structural.allowed()) return structural;
  65:

  68:             TradeSignal previous = previousSignal(executionSignal.getSymbol(), EXECUTION_INTERVAL, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt()).orElse(null);
  69:             // FIX-112A: a bullish label alone is not a consumed entry. Only keep
  70:             // BUY_CONTINUATION protection when that exact previous signal has an
  71:             // executed BUY (Production) or executed shadow BUY (Replay).
  72:             if (isFresh(previous, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_MINUTE_TRANSITION_MAX_AGE)

  77:                                 + "A new WATCH/NEUTRAL/SELL -> BUY transition is required.");
  78:             }
  79:         }
  80: 
  81:         TradeSignal fiveMinute = latestAtOrBefore(executionSignal, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL);
  82:         if (!isFresh(fiveMinute, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE)) {
  83:             return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_5M_CONFIRMATION",
  84:                     "No fresh 5m confirmation was available for the 1m BUY signal.");
  85:         }
  86: 
  87:         TradeSignal oneHour = latestAtOrBefore(executionSignal, TREND_INTERVAL);
  88:         if (!isFresh(oneHour, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE)) {
  89:             return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_1H_CONTEXT",
  90:                     "No fresh 1h strategic context was available for the 1m BUY signal.");
  91:         }
  92: 
  93:         HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment=htfAssessment.assess(fiveMinute,true,oneHour,true);
  94:         if(!assessment.allowed()) {

  96:             audit(executionSignal, "DIRECT_BUY", assessment, assessment.code(), "FIX-154 higher-timeframe assessment rejected this BUY.");
  97:             return ValidationResult.reject(assessment.code(), "FIX-154 higher-timeframe assessment rejected this BUY.");
  98:         }
  99: 
 100:         ExecutionProfile profile = ExecutionProfile.from(settings.getExecutionProfile());
 101:         ValidationResult profileResult = switch (profile) {
 102:             case CONSERVATIVE -> conservativeBuy(assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour());
 103:             // FIX-112B: only the fresh direct-BUY BALANCED path receives the narrow
 104:             // 5m-NEUTRAL exception. validateBuyContext() and AGGRESSIVE fallback keep
 105:             // calling balancedBuy() directly so their proven Production behavior is unchanged.
 106:             case BALANCED -> balancedBuyWithNeutralFiveException(
 107:                     executionSignal, assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour(), entryQualityScore);
 108:             case AGGRESSIVE -> aggressiveBuy(executionSignal, assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour());
 109:         };
 110:         ValidationResult result = cap(profileResult, assessment);
 111:         audit(executionSignal, "DIRECT_BUY", assessment, result.code(), result.explanation());
 112:         return result;
 113:     }

 116:      * FIX-021 / accumulated-evidence authority parity:

 118:      * execution profile as a normal direct BUY. This intentionally skips the 1m BUY

 128:     public ValidationResult validateBuyContext(TradeSignal referenceSignal, String auditPath) {
 129:         if (referenceSignal == null || referenceSignal.getGeneratedAt() == null) {
 130:             return ValidationResult.reject("INVALID_SIGNAL", "Execution reference signal is missing required timing data.");
 131:         }
 132: 
 133:         TradeSignal fiveMinute = latestAtOrBefore(referenceSignal, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL);
 134:         if (!isFresh(fiveMinute, referenceSignal.getGeneratedAt(), FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE)) {
 135:             return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_5M_CONFIRMATION",
 136:                     "No fresh 5m confirmation was available for accumulated-evidence authority.");
 137:         }
 138: 
 139:         TradeSignal oneHour = latestAtOrBefore(referenceSignal, TREND_INTERVAL);
 140:         if (!isFresh(oneHour, referenceSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE)) {
 141:             return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_1H_CONTEXT",
 142:                     "No fresh 1h strategic context was available for accumulated-evidence authority.");
 143:         }
 144: 
 145:         HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment=htfAssessment.assess(fiveMinute,true,oneHour,true);
 146:         if(!assessment.allowed()) {
 147:             recordAssessmentMetric(assessment);
 148:             audit(referenceSignal, auditPath, assessment, assessment.code(), "FIX-154 higher-timeframe assessment rejected this BUY context.");
 149:             return ValidationResult.reject(assessment.code(), "FIX-154 higher-timeframe assessment rejected this BUY context.");
 150:         }
 151: 
 152:         ExecutionProfile profile = ExecutionProfile.from(settings().getExecutionProfile());
 153:         TradeSignal oneMinuteQuality = latestAtOrBefore(referenceSignal, EXECUTION_INTERVAL);
 154:         TradeSignal qualitySignal = oneMinuteQuality == null ? referenceSignal : oneMinuteQuality;
 155:         ValidationResult profileResult = switch (profile) {
 156:             case CONSERVATIVE -> conservativeBuy(assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour());
 157:             case BALANCED -> balancedBuy(assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour());
 158:             case AGGRESSIVE -> aggressiveBuy(qualitySignal, assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour());
 159:         };
 160:         ValidationResult result = cap(profileResult, assessment);
 161:         audit(referenceSignal, auditPath, assessment, result.code(), result.explanation());
 162:         return result;

 165:     public ValidationResult validateSell(TradeSignal executionSignal) {
 166:         ValidationResult base = validateBaseSignal(executionSignal, false);
 167:         if (!base.allowed()) return base;
 168: 
 169:         TradeSignal fiveMinute = latestAtOrBefore(executionSignal, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL);
 170:         if (!isFresh(fiveMinute, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE)) {
 171:             return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_5M_CONFIRMATION",
 172:                     "No fresh 5m confirmation was available for the 1m SELL signal.");
 173:         }
 174:         if (!isBearish(fiveMinute.getDecision())) {
 175:             return ValidationResult.reject("5M_NOT_BEARISH",
 176:                     "1m SELL was blocked because the fresh 5m decision was " + fiveMinute.getDecision() + ".");
 177:         }
 178: 
 179:         TradeSignal oneHour = latestAtOrBefore(executionSignal, TREND_INTERVAL);
 180:         if (!isFresh(oneHour, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE)) {
 181:             return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_1H_CONTEXT",
 182:                     "No fresh 1h strategic context was available for the 1m SELL signal.");
 183:         }
 184:         if (isBullish(oneHour.getDecision())) {
 185:             return ValidationResult.reject("1H_BULLISH_VETO",
 186:                     "1m SELL was blocked because the fresh 1h strategic decision was " + oneHour.getDecision() + ".");
 187:         }
 188: 
 189:         return ValidationResult.allow(100, "SELL_CONFIRMED",
 190:                 "1m SELL confirmed by fresh bearish 5m context; 1h is not bullish.");
 191:     }

 193:     private ValidationResult conservativeBuy(SignalDecision fiveMinute, SignalDecision oneHour) {
 194:         if (!isBullish(fiveMinute)) {
 195:             return ValidationResult.reject("5M_NOT_BULLISH",
 196:                     "Conservative profile requires 5m BUY/STRONG_BUY; fresh 5m is " + fiveMinute + ".");
 197:         }
 198:         return ValidationResult.allow(100, "CONSERVATIVE_FULL",
 199:                 "Conservative profile approved a full-size entry: 1m BUY, bullish 5m confirmation, and non-bearish 1h context.");
 200:     }

 202:     /**
 203:      * FIX-112B / Production behavior change:
 204:      * 5m NEUTRAL means short-term confirmation is absent, but it is not bearish
 205:      * opposition. After the existing BALANCED rules reject, allow only a small
 206:      * exploratory entry when the fresh 1m BUY is high-confidence, 1h is genuinely
 207:      * bullish, independent Entry Quality is strong, and BTC has no conflict state.
 208:      *
 209:      * Golden rule: Replay = Production. This method lives in the shared Production
 210:      * validation service and Replay must call this exact path with historical inputs;
 211:      * there is no Replay-only copy, threshold, or behavior fork.
 212:      *
 213:      * balancedBuy() intentionally remains unchanged. Existing BALANCED_FULL,
 214:      * BALANCED_STRONG and BALANCED_EARLY authority always wins before this exception.
 215:      * FIX-11G extends this same direct-BUY-only wrapper after FIX-112B with the distinct
 216:      * 5m=NEUTRAL + 1h=WATCH transitional authority documented below.
 217:      */
 218:     private ValidationResult balancedBuyWithNeutralFiveException(
 219:             TradeSignal executionSignal, SignalDecision fiveMinute, SignalDecision oneHour, int entryQualityScore) {
 220:         ValidationResult balanced = balancedBuy(fiveMinute, oneHour);
 221:         if (balanced.allowed()) return balanced;
 222: 
 223:         boolean neutralFiveBullishOne = fiveMinute == SignalDecision.NEUTRAL && isBullish(oneHour);
 224:         BtcContextStatus btcStatus = executionSignal.getBtcContextStatus();
 225:         // FIX-112B: plain/strong BTC conflict cannot be rescued by this exception.
 226:         // FIX-141: nor can a failed freshness guard - STALE_CONTEXT is a real, evidenced
 227:         // block (missing/stale/future-dated/unknown-candle-time BTC context), not an
 228:         // "unavailable, non-authoritative" state. This exception must not restore the
 229:         // entry permission the freshness guard already withheld upstream.
 230:         // Other BTC states retain their existing upstream Production semantics.
 231:         boolean btcSafeForException = btcStatus != BtcContextStatus.CONFLICT
 232:                 && btcStatus != BtcContextStatus.STRONG_CONFLICT
 233:                 && btcStatus != BtcContextStatus.STALE_CONTEXT;
 234:         boolean strongEnough = executionSignal.getConfidenceScore() >= 72
 235:                 && entryQualityScore >= 70
 236:                 && btcSafeForException;
 237: 
 238:         if (neutralFiveBullishOne && strongEnough) {
 239:             return ValidationResult.allow(25, "BALANCED_NEUTRAL_5M",
 240:                     "BALANCED_NEUTRAL_5M: 25% exploratory entry approved because 5m is NEUTRAL "
 241:                              + "rather than bearish while 1h remains " + oneHour
 242:                             + ". 1m=" + executionSignal.getDecision()
 243:                             + ", confidence=" + executionSignal.getConfidenceScore()
 244:                              + ", 5m=NEUTRAL, 1h=" + oneHour
 245:                             + ", entryQuality=" + entryQualityScore
 246:                             + ", btcStatus=" + btcStatus
 247:                             + ", position=25%.");
 248:         }
 249: 
 250:         // FIX-11G / controlled Production behavior change:
 251:         // An already-approved fresh 1m BUY can appear before tactical 5m confirmation
 252:         // catches up. When 5m is still NEUTRAL and 1h is WATCH, neither timeframe is
 253:         // bearish, but the original BALANCED matrix has no authority for this transition.
 254:         // Grant only a 25% exploratory authority and only when FinalDecision already
 255:         // allowed entry, BTC has no hard conflict, and Entry Quality is at/above the
 256:         // existing chase cutoff. Do NOT add a second confidence threshold here: the
 257:         // upstream BUY + finalEntryAllowed decision already owns signal confidence.
 258:         //
 259:         // Golden rule: Replay = Production. Replay reaches this exact shared method
 260:         // through ExecutionReplayScope historical 5m/1h lookups; there is no Replay-only
 261:         // copy, threshold, or special execution behavior. validateBuyContext() remains
 262:         // intentionally unchanged so accumulated-evidence/recovery authority is not widened.
 263:         boolean neutralFiveWatchOne = fiveMinute == SignalDecision.NEUTRAL && oneHour == SignalDecision.WATCH;
 264:         if (neutralFiveWatchOne) {
 265:             boolean upstreamApproved = executionSignal.isFinalEntryAllowed();
 266:             boolean entryQualitySafe = entryQualityScore >= BALANCED_TRANSITIONAL_MIN_ENTRY_QUALITY;
 267:             // FIX-141: see btcSafeForException above - STALE_CONTEXT must not be rescued here either.
 268:             boolean btcSafeForTransitionalAuthority = btcStatus != BtcContextStatus.CONFLICT
 269:                     && btcStatus != BtcContextStatus.STRONG_CONFLICT
 270:                     && btcStatus != BtcContextStatus.STALE_CONTEXT;
 271: 
 272:             if (upstreamApproved && entryQualitySafe && btcSafeForTransitionalAuthority) {
 273:                 log.info("FIX-11G transitional BUY authority granted: signalId={}, symbol={}, generatedAt={}, "
 274:                                 + "decision={}, confidence={}, 5m={}, 1h={}, entryQuality={}, btcStatus={}, positionPercent=25",
 275:                         executionSignal.getId(), executionSignal.getSymbol(), executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(),
 276:                         executionSignal.getDecision(), executionSignal.getConfidenceScore(),
 277:                         fiveMinute, oneHour, entryQualityScore, btcStatus);
 278:                 return ValidationResult.allow(25, "BALANCED_NEUTRAL_5M_WATCH_1H",
 279:                         "BALANCED_NEUTRAL_5M_WATCH_1H: 25% exploratory entry approved for an already-approved "
 280:                                 + "1m BUY while 5m is NEUTRAL and 1h is WATCH. "
 281:                                 + "entryQuality=" + entryQualityScore
 282:                                 + ", btcStatus=" + btcStatus
 283:                                 + ", position=25%.");
 284:             }
 285: 
 286:             // INFO is intentional here: this exact transition is the new FIX-11G boundary.
 287:             // Keeping both grant and denial searchable by FIX-11G makes Production misses
 288:             // diagnosable from application logs without enabling global DEBUG logging.
 289:             log.info("FIX-11G transitional BUY authority not granted: signalId={}, symbol={}, generatedAt={}, "
 290:                             + "decision={}, confidence={}, finalEntryAllowed={}, 5m={}, 1h={}, entryQuality={}, "
 291:                             + "minEntryQuality={}, btcStatus={}",
 292:                     executionSignal.getId(), executionSignal.getSymbol(), executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(),
 293:                     executionSignal.getDecision(), executionSignal.getConfidenceScore(), upstreamApproved,
 294:                     fiveMinute, oneHour, entryQualityScore,
 295:                     BALANCED_TRANSITIONAL_MIN_ENTRY_QUALITY, btcStatus);
 296:         }
 297:         return balanced;
 298:     }
