 300:     private ValidationResult balancedBuy(SignalDecision five, SignalDecision one) {
 301: 
 302:         if (isBullish(five) && isBullish(one)) {
 303:             return ValidationResult.allow(100, "BALANCED_FULL",
 304:                     "Balanced profile approved 100%: both 5m and 1h are bullish.");
 305:         }
 306:         if (isBullish(five) && isWatchOrNeutral(one)) {
 307:             return ValidationResult.allow(75, "BALANCED_STRONG",
 308:                     "Balanced profile approved 75%: 5m is bullish while 1h is " + one + ".");
 309:         }
 310:         if (five == SignalDecision.WATCH && isBullish(one)) {
 311:             return ValidationResult.allow(75, "BALANCED_STRONG",
 312:                     "Balanced profile approved 75%: 1h is bullish while 5m is WATCH.");
 313:         }
 314:         if (five == SignalDecision.WATCH && one == SignalDecision.WATCH) {
 315:             return ValidationResult.allow(50, "BALANCED_EARLY",
 316:                     "Balanced profile approved 50%: both 5m and 1h are WATCH, so this is an early reduced entry.");
 317:         }
 318: 
 319:         return ValidationResult.reject("BALANCED_CONFIRMATION_INSUFFICIENT",
 320:                 "Balanced profile requires bullish/WATCH confirmation. Current 5m=" + five + ", 1h=" + one + ".");
 321:     }
 322: 
 323:     private ValidationResult aggressiveBuy(TradeSignal executionSignal, SignalDecision five, SignalDecision one) {
 324:         ValidationResult balanced = balancedBuy(five, one);
 325:         if (balanced.allowed()) return balanced;
 326: 
 327:         boolean probeAlignment = five == SignalDecision.WATCH && one == SignalDecision.NEUTRAL;
 328:         if (!probeAlignment) {
 329:             return ValidationResult.reject("AGGRESSIVE_CONFIRMATION_INSUFFICIENT",
 330:                     "Aggressive profile still requires 5m WATCH or better and a non-bearish 1h context. Current 5m="
 331:                             + five + ", 1h=" + one + ".");
 332:         }
 333: 
 334:         int confidence = executionSignal.getConfidenceScore();
 335:         int trend = executionSignal.getTrendScore();
 336:         int volume = executionSignal.getVolumeScore();
 337:         int momentum = executionSignal.getMomentumScore();
 338: 
 339:         if (confidence < 85 || trend < 22 || volume < 16 || momentum < 12) {
 340:             return ValidationResult.reject("AGGRESSIVE_PROBE_QUALITY",
 341:                     "Aggressive 25% probe requires confidence>=85, trend>=22, volume>=16, momentum>=12. "
 342:                             + "Actual confidence=" + confidence + ", trend=" + trend + ", volume=" + volume
 343:                             + ", momentum=" + momentum + ".");
 344:         }
 345: 
 346:         return ValidationResult.allow(25, "AGGRESSIVE_PROBE",
 347:                 "Aggressive profile approved a 25% probe: 1m BUY is high quality, 5m is WATCH, and 1h is NEUTRAL.");
 348:     }
 349: 
 350:     private ValidationResult validateBaseSignal(TradeSignal signal, boolean buy) {
 351:         if (signal == null || signal.getGeneratedAt() == null) {
 352:             return ValidationResult.reject("INVALID_SIGNAL", "Execution signal is missing required timing data.");
 353:         }
 354:         if (!EXECUTION_INTERVAL.equals(signal.getInterval())) {
 355:             return ValidationResult.reject("NON_EXECUTION_TIMEFRAME",
 356:                     "Only 1m signals may trigger normal wallet execution. " + signal.getInterval() + " is context-only.");
 357:         }
 358:         if (buy && !isBullish(signal.getDecision())) {
 359:             return ValidationResult.reject("NOT_BUY", "The 1m decision is not BUY/STRONG_BUY.");
 360:         }
 361:         if (!buy && !isBearish(signal.getDecision())) {
 362:             return ValidationResult.reject("NOT_SELL", "The 1m decision is not SELL/STRONG_SELL.");
 363:         }
 364:         return ValidationResult.allow(100, "BASE_VALID", "Base execution signal is valid.");
 365:     }
 366: 
 367:     private TradeSignal latestAtOrBefore(TradeSignal signal, String interval) {
 368:         return latestSignal(signal.getSymbol(), interval, signal.getGeneratedAt()).orElse(null);
 369:     }
 370: 
 371:     private java.util.Optional<TradeSignal> previousSignal(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference) {
 372:         if (replayScope != null && replayScope.active()) return replayScope.previousBefore(symbol, interval, reference);
 373:         return signalRepository.findTopBySymbolAndIntervalAndGeneratedAtLessThanOrderByGeneratedAtDesc(symbol, interval, reference);
 374:     }

 376:     private java.util.Optional<TradeSignal> latestSignal(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference) {
 377:         if (replayScope != null && replayScope.active()) return replayScope.latestAtOrBefore(symbol, interval, reference);
 378:         return signalRepository.findTopBySymbolAndIntervalAndGeneratedAtLessThanEqualOrderByGeneratedAtDesc(symbol, interval, reference);
 379:     }

 381:     private boolean isFresh(TradeSignal signal, Instant reference, Duration maximumAge) {
 382:         return signal != null
 383:                 && signal.getGeneratedAt() != null
 384:                 && !signal.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(reference)
 385:                 && !signal.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(reference.minus(maximumAge));
 386:     }

 390:     }

 393:         return decision == SignalDecision.SELL || decision == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL;
 394:     }

 397:         return decision == SignalDecision.WATCH || decision == SignalDecision.NEUTRAL;

 414:         if (!result.allowed() || assessment.maxInitialPercent()==null) return result;
 415:         int capped=Math.min(result.positionPercent(),assessment.maxInitialPercent());
 416:         if (capped != result.positionPercent() && fix154Metrics != null) fix154Metrics.softCappedEntry();

 420:     /** Used only after progressive raw-HTF checks have already failed; never changes an add decision. */
 421:     public void recordProgressiveAssessmentIfSoftBlocked(TradeSignal referenceSignal) {
 422:         if (referenceSignal == null || referenceSignal.getGeneratedAt() == null) return;
 423:         TradeSignal five = latestAtOrBefore(referenceSignal, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL);
 424:         TradeSignal one = latestAtOrBefore(referenceSignal, TREND_INTERVAL);
 425:         if (!isFresh(five, referenceSignal.getGeneratedAt(), FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE)
 426:                 || !isFresh(one, referenceSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE)) return;
 427:         HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment = htfAssessment.assess(five, true, one, true);
 428:         if (assessment.band() == HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Band.SOFT_CONFLICT) {
 429:             if (fix154Metrics != null) fix154Metrics.softAddBlocked();
 430:             audit(referenceSignal, "PROGRESSIVE_ADD", assessment, "SOFT_HTF_ADD_BLOCKED",
 431:                     "Existing raw 1h progressive-add guard blocked this add; FIX-154 audit only.");
 432:         }
 433:     }

 435:     private void audit(TradeSignal referenceSignal, String path,
 436:                        HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment, String finalResult, String reason) {
 437:         if (htfAuditStore == null) return;
 438:         try {
 439:             htfAuditStore.record(referenceSignal, path, assessment, finalResult, reason);
 440:         } catch (RuntimeException auditFailure) {
 441:             if (fix154Metrics != null) fix154Metrics.auditWriteFailed();
 442:             log.warn("FIX-154 audit handoff failed; BUY decision remains unchanged: path={}, signalId={}",
 443:                     path, referenceSignal == null ? null : referenceSignal.getId(), auditFailure);
 444:         }
 445:     }

 449:                 && assessment.band() == HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Band.NOT_TRUSTED) {
 450:             fix154Metrics.untrustedBearishOneHourBlocked();
 451:         }

  24:     /**
  25:      * Builds the neutral ATR risk snapshot before strategy selection.
  26:      */
  27:     public AtrRiskAssessment assess(IndicatorSnapshot indicator) {
  28:         BigDecimal price = requirePositive(indicator.latestPrice(), "Latest price");
  29:         BigDecimal atr = requirePositive(indicator.atr14(), "ATR14");
  30: 
  31:         BigDecimal atrPercent = atr.divide(price, 10, RoundingMode.HALF_UP)
  32:                 .multiply(ONE_HUNDRED);
  33: 
  34:         BigDecimal configuredStopDistance = atr.multiply(properties.stopLossMultiplier(), MC);
  35:         BigDecimal minimumStopDistance = price.multiply(properties.minimumStopPercent(), MC)
  36:                 .divide(ONE_HUNDRED, MC);
  37:         BigDecimal maximumStopDistance = price.multiply(properties.maximumStopPercent(), MC)
  38:                 .divide(ONE_HUNDRED, MC);
  39: 
  40:         BigDecimal stopDistance = configuredStopDistance.max(minimumStopDistance).min(maximumStopDistance);
  41:         BigDecimal rewardDistance = atr.multiply(properties.takeProfitMultiplier(), MC);
  42: 
  43:         BigDecimal stopLoss = price.subtract(stopDistance, MC).max(BigDecimal.ZERO);
  44:         BigDecimal takeProfit = price.add(rewardDistance, MC);
  45:         BigDecimal riskRewardRatio = rewardDistance.divide(stopDistance, 6, RoundingMode.HALF_UP);
  46: 
  47:         BigDecimal distanceFromSma = indicator.sma20() == null
  48:                 ? BigDecimal.ZERO
  49:                 : price.subtract(indicator.sma20()).abs();
  50:         BigDecimal distanceAtrMultiple = distanceFromSma.divide(atr, 6, RoundingMode.HALF_UP);
  51:         boolean overextended = distanceAtrMultiple.compareTo(properties.overextensionMultiplier()) >= 0;
  52: 
  53:         String volatilityLevel = volatilityLevel(atrPercent);
  54:         String explanation = baseExplanation(atr, atrPercent, volatilityLevel)
  55:                 + (overextended ? " Price is extended from SMA20 by "
  56:                 + distanceAtrMultiple.setScale(2, RoundingMode.HALF_UP) + " ATR." : "");
  57: 
  58:         return new AtrRiskAssessment(
  59:                 atr,
  60:                 atrPercent,
  61:                 stopLoss,
  62:                 takeProfit,
  63:                 stopDistance,
  64:                 rewardDistance,
  65:                 riskRewardRatio,
  66:                 distanceAtrMultiple,
  67:                 volatilityLevel,
  68:                 overextended,
  69:                 AtrEntryType.STANDARD_ENTRY,
  70:                 100,
  71:                 true,
  72:                 price,
  73:                 explanation
  74:         );
  75:     }
  76: 
  77:     /**
  78:      * Converts ATR extension into a strategy-aware entry plan. Direction is preserved unless
  79:      * extension exceeds the hard-veto level. Pullback and retracement plans keep BUY as BUY,
  80:      * but prevent an immediate market entry until a better price is reached.
  81:      */
  82:     public AtrRiskAssessment applyStrategyEntryPlan(
  83:             AtrRiskAssessment base,
  84:             IndicatorSnapshot indicator,
  85:             TradingStrategy strategy
  86:     ) {
  87:         BigDecimal distance = base.candleRangeAtrMultiple();
  88:         Thresholds thresholds = thresholds(strategy);
  89: 
  90:         AtrEntryType entryType;
  91:         int positionPercent;
  92:         boolean immediateEntryAllowed;
  93: 
  94:         if (distance.compareTo(thresholds.reduced()) < 0) {
  95:             entryType = AtrEntryType.STANDARD_ENTRY;
  96:             positionPercent = 100;
  97:             immediateEntryAllowed = true;
  98:         } else if (distance.compareTo(thresholds.pullback()) < 0) {
  99:             entryType = AtrEntryType.REDUCED_POSITION;
 100:             positionPercent = properties.reducedPositionPercent();
 101:             immediateEntryAllowed = true;
 102:         } else if (distance.compareTo(thresholds.waitForRetracement()) < 0) {
 103:             entryType = AtrEntryType.PULLBACK_ENTRY;
 104:             positionPercent = properties.reducedPositionPercent();
 105:             immediateEntryAllowed = false;
 106:         } else if (distance.compareTo(thresholds.hardVeto()) < 0) {
 107:             entryType = AtrEntryType.WAIT_FOR_RETRACEMENT;
 108:             positionPercent = 0;
 109:             immediateEntryAllowed = false;
 110:         } else {
 111:             entryType = AtrEntryType.NO_ENTRY;
 112:             positionPercent = 0;
 113:             immediateEntryAllowed = false;
 114:         }
 115: 
 116:         BigDecimal retracementEntryPrice = retracementEntryPrice(indicator, base.atr(), thresholds.reduced());
 117:         String explanation = baseExplanation(base.atr(), base.atrPercent(), base.volatilityLevel())
 118:                 + " Price is " + distance.setScale(2, RoundingMode.HALF_UP)
 119:                 + " ATR from SMA20. Strategy-aware entry type is " + entryType
 120:                 + ". Recommended position is " + positionPercent + "%"
 121:                 + (immediateEntryAllowed ? ". Immediate entry remains allowed."
 122:                 : ". Wait for price to retrace near " + retracementEntryPrice.stripTrailingZeros().toPlainString() + ".");
 123: 
 124:         return new AtrRiskAssessment(
 125:                 base.atr(),
 126:                 base.atrPercent(),
 127:                 base.stopLoss(),
 128:                 base.takeProfit(),
 129:                 base.stopDistance(),
 130:                 base.rewardDistance(),
 131:                 base.riskRewardRatio(),
 132:                 base.candleRangeAtrMultiple(),
 133:                 base.volatilityLevel(),
 134:                 entryType != AtrEntryType.STANDARD_ENTRY,
 135:                 entryType,
 136:                 positionPercent,
 137:                 immediateEntryAllowed,
 138:                 retracementEntryPrice,
 139:                 explanation
 140:         );
 141:     }
 142: 
 143:     private Thresholds thresholds(TradingStrategy strategy) {
 144:         BigDecimal reduced = properties.reducedPositionMultiplier();
 145:         BigDecimal pullback = properties.pullbackEntryMultiplier();
 146:         BigDecimal waitForRetracement = properties.waitForRetracementMultiplier();
 147:         BigDecimal hardVeto = properties.hardVetoMultiplier();
 148: 
 149:         if (strategy == TradingStrategy.BREAKOUT) {
 150:             return new Thresholds(
 151:                     reduced.add(new BigDecimal("0.50")),
 152:                     pullback.add(new BigDecimal("0.75")),
 153:                     waitForRetracement.add(new BigDecimal("0.75")),
 154:                     hardVeto.add(new BigDecimal("0.75"))
 155:             );
 156:         }
 157:         if (strategy == TradingStrategy.RANGE_MEAN_REVERSION) {
 158:             return new Thresholds(
 159:                     reduced.subtract(new BigDecimal("0.50")).max(new BigDecimal("1.00")),
 160:                     pullback.subtract(new BigDecimal("0.75")).max(new BigDecimal("1.50")),
 161:                     waitForRetracement.subtract(new BigDecimal("1.00")).max(new BigDecimal("2.00")),
 162:                     hardVeto.subtract(new BigDecimal("1.25")).max(new BigDecimal("2.50"))
 163:             );
 164:         }
 165:         if (strategy == TradingStrategy.DEFENSIVE || strategy == TradingStrategy.NO_TRADE) {
 166:             return new Thresholds(
 167:                     reduced.subtract(new BigDecimal("0.50")).max(new BigDecimal("1.00")),
 168:                     pullback.subtract(new BigDecimal("0.50")).max(new BigDecimal("1.75")),
 169:                     waitForRetracement.subtract(new BigDecimal("0.75")).max(new BigDecimal("2.50")),
 170:                     hardVeto.subtract(new BigDecimal("1.00")).max(new BigDecimal("3.00"))
 171:             );
 172:         }
 173:         return new Thresholds(reduced, pullback, waitForRetracement, hardVeto);
 174:     }

 176:     private BigDecimal retracementEntryPrice(
 177:             IndicatorSnapshot indicator,
 178:             BigDecimal atr,
 179:             BigDecimal acceptableDistanceAtr

 181:         if (indicator.sma20() == null) {
 182:             return indicator.latestPrice();
 183:         }
 184:         BigDecimal acceptableDistance = atr.multiply(acceptableDistanceAtr, MC);
 185:         return indicator.sma20().add(acceptableDistance, MC);
 186:     }

 192:                 + properties.stopLossMultiplier() + " ATR and target uses "

 196:     private String volatilityLevel(BigDecimal atrPercent) {
 197:         if (atrPercent.compareTo(properties.extremeVolatilityPercent()) >= 0) return "EXTREME";
 198:         if (atrPercent.compareTo(properties.highVolatilityPercent()) >= 0) return "HIGH";
 199:         if (atrPercent.compareTo(properties.lowVolatilityPercent()) < 0) return "LOW";
 200:         return "NORMAL";

 206:         }

  68:             TradeSignal previous = previousSignal(executionSignal.getSymbol(), EXECUTION_INTERVAL, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt()).orElse(null);
  69:             // FIX-112A: a bullish label alone is not a consumed entry. Only keep
  70:             // BUY_CONTINUATION protection when that exact previous signal has an
  71:             // executed BUY (Production) or executed shadow BUY (Replay).
  72:             if (isFresh(previous, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_MINUTE_TRANSITION_MAX_AGE)
  73:                     && isBullish(previous.getDecision())
  74:                     && entryConsumptionPolicy.resolve(previous.getId()) != EntryConsumptionState.NOT_CONSUMED) {
  75:                 return ValidationResult.reject("BUY_CONTINUATION",
  76:                         "BUY was not executed because a recent bullish opportunity for this symbol was already consumed. "
  77:                                 + "A new WATCH/NEUTRAL/SELL -> BUY transition is required.");
  78:             }
  79:         }
  80: 
  81:         TradeSignal fiveMinute = latestAtOrBefore(executionSignal, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL);
  82:         if (!isFresh(fiveMinute, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE)) {
  83:             return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_5M_CONFIRMATION",
  84:                     "No fresh 5m confirmation was available for the 1m BUY signal.");
  85:         }
  86: 
  87:         TradeSignal oneHour = latestAtOrBefore(executionSignal, TREND_INTERVAL);
  88:         if (!isFresh(oneHour, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE)) {
  89:             return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_1H_CONTEXT",
  90:                     "No fresh 1h strategic context was available for the 1m BUY signal.");
  91:         }
  92: 
  93:         HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment=htfAssessment.assess(fiveMinute,true,oneHour,true);
  94:         if(!assessment.allowed()) {
  95:             recordAssessmentMetric(assessment);
  96:             audit(executionSignal, "DIRECT_BUY", assessment, assessment.code(), "FIX-154 higher-timeframe assessment rejected this BUY.");
  97:             return ValidationResult.reject(assessment.code(), "FIX-154 higher-timeframe assessment rejected this BUY.");
  98:         }
  99: 
 100:         ExecutionProfile profile = ExecutionProfile.from(settings.getExecutionProfile());
 101:         ValidationResult profileResult = switch (profile) {
 102:             case CONSERVATIVE -> conservativeBuy(assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour());
 103:             // FIX-112B: only the fresh direct-BUY BALANCED path receives the narrow
 104:             // 5m-NEUTRAL exception. validateBuyContext() and AGGRESSIVE fallback keep
 105:             // calling balancedBuy() directly so their proven Production behavior is unchanged.
 106:             case BALANCED -> balancedBuyWithNeutralFiveException(
 107:                     executionSignal, assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour(), entryQualityScore);
 108:             case AGGRESSIVE -> aggressiveBuy(executionSignal, assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour());
 109:         };
 110:         ValidationResult result = cap(profileResult, assessment);
 111:         audit(executionSignal, "DIRECT_BUY", assessment, result.code(), result.explanation());
 112:         return result;
 113:     }

 115:     /**
 116:      * FIX-021 / accumulated-evidence authority parity:
 117:      * Validate only the current 5m/1h execution authority using the same configured
 118:      * execution profile as a normal direct BUY. This intentionally skips the 1m BUY
 119:      * transition requirement because accumulated evidence may be triggered by a WATCH,
 120:      * but it must never be allowed to use weaker higher-timeframe authority than the
 121:      * normal BUY path would accept.
 122:      */
 123:     public ValidationResult validateBuyContext(TradeSignal referenceSignal) {
 124:         return validateBuyContext(referenceSignal, "ACCUMULATED_EVIDENCE");
 125:     }

 127:     /** The caller path is audit evidence only; context eligibility remains identical. */
 128:     public ValidationResult validateBuyContext(TradeSignal referenceSignal, String auditPath) {
 129:         if (referenceSignal == null || referenceSignal.getGeneratedAt() == null) {
 130:             return ValidationResult.reject("INVALID_SIGNAL", "Execution reference signal is missing required timing data.");
 131:         }
 132: 
 133:         TradeSignal fiveMinute = latestAtOrBefore(referenceSignal, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL);
 134:         if (!isFresh(fiveMinute, referenceSignal.getGeneratedAt(), FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE)) {
 135:             return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_5M_CONFIRMATION",
 136:                     "No fresh 5m confirmation was available for accumulated-evidence authority.");
 137:         }
 138: 
 139:         TradeSignal oneHour = latestAtOrBefore(referenceSignal, TREND_INTERVAL);
 140:         if (!isFresh(oneHour, referenceSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE)) {
 141:             return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_1H_CONTEXT",
 142:                     "No fresh 1h strategic context was available for accumulated-evidence authority.");
 143:         }
 144: 
 145:         HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment=htfAssessment.assess(fiveMinute,true,oneHour,true);
 146:         if(!assessment.allowed()) {
 147:             recordAssessmentMetric(assessment);
 148:             audit(referenceSignal, auditPath, assessment, assessment.code(), "FIX-154 higher-timeframe assessment rejected this BUY context.");
 149:             return ValidationResult.reject(assessment.code(), "FIX-154 higher-timeframe assessment rejected this BUY context.");
 150:         }
 151: 
 152:         ExecutionProfile profile = ExecutionProfile.from(settings().getExecutionProfile());
 153:         TradeSignal oneMinuteQuality = latestAtOrBefore(referenceSignal, EXECUTION_INTERVAL);
 154:         TradeSignal qualitySignal = oneMinuteQuality == null ? referenceSignal : oneMinuteQuality;
 155:         ValidationResult profileResult = switch (profile) {
 156:             case CONSERVATIVE -> conservativeBuy(assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour());
 157:             case BALANCED -> balancedBuy(assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour());
 158:             case AGGRESSIVE -> aggressiveBuy(qualitySignal, assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour());
 159:         };
 160:         ValidationResult result = cap(profileResult, assessment);
 161:         audit(referenceSignal, auditPath, assessment, result.code(), result.explanation());
 162:         return result;
 163:     }

 165:     public ValidationResult validateSell(TradeSignal executionSignal) {
 166:         ValidationResult base = validateBaseSignal(executionSignal, false);
 167:         if (!base.allowed()) return base;
 168: 
 169:         TradeSignal fiveMinute = latestAtOrBefore(executionSignal, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL);
 170:         if (!isFresh(fiveMinute, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE)) {
 171:             return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_5M_CONFIRMATION",
 172:                     "No fresh 5m confirmation was available for the 1m SELL signal.");
 173:         }
 174:         if (!isBearish(fiveMinute.getDecision())) {
 175:             return ValidationResult.reject("5M_NOT_BEARISH",
 176:                     "1m SELL was blocked because the fresh 5m decision was " + fiveMinute.getDecision() + ".");
 177:         }
 178: 
 179:         TradeSignal oneHour = latestAtOrBefore(executionSignal, TREND_INTERVAL);
 180:         if (!isFresh(oneHour, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE)) {
 181:             return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_1H_CONTEXT",
 182:                     "No fresh 1h strategic context was available for the 1m SELL signal.");
 183:         }
 184:         if (isBullish(oneHour.getDecision())) {
 185:             return ValidationResult.reject("1H_BULLISH_VETO",
 186:                     "1m SELL was blocked because the fresh 1h strategic decision was " + oneHour.getDecision() + ".");
 187:         }
 188: 
 189:         return ValidationResult.allow(100, "SELL_CONFIRMED",
 190:                 "1m SELL confirmed by fresh bearish 5m context; 1h is not bullish.");
 191:     }
 192: 
 193:     private ValidationResult conservativeBuy(SignalDecision fiveMinute, SignalDecision oneHour) {
 194:         if (!isBullish(fiveMinute)) {
 195:             return ValidationResult.reject("5M_NOT_BULLISH",
 196:                     "Conservative profile requires 5m BUY/STRONG_BUY; fresh 5m is " + fiveMinute + ".");
 197:         }
 198:         return ValidationResult.allow(100, "CONSERVATIVE_FULL",
 199:                 "Conservative profile approved a full-size entry: 1m BUY, bullish 5m confirmation, and non-bearish 1h context.");
 200:     }
 201: 
 202:     /**
 203:      * FIX-112B / Production behavior change:
 204:      * 5m NEUTRAL means short-term confirmation is absent, but it is not bearish
 205:      * opposition. After the existing BALANCED rules reject, allow only a small
 206:      * exploratory entry when the fresh 1m BUY is high-confidence, 1h is genuinely
 207:      * bullish, independent Entry Quality is strong, and BTC has no conflict state.
 208:      *
 209:      * Golden rule: Replay = Production. This method lives in the shared Production
 210:      * validation service and Replay must call this exact path with historical inputs;
 211:      * there is no Replay-only copy, threshold, or behavior fork.
 212:      *
 213:      * balancedBuy() intentionally remains unchanged. Existing BALANCED_FULL,
 214:      * BALANCED_STRONG and BALANCED_EARLY authority always wins before this exception.
 215:      * FIX-11G extends this same direct-BUY-only wrapper after FIX-112B with the distinct
 216:      * 5m=NEUTRAL + 1h=WATCH transitional authority documented below.
 217:      */
 218:     private ValidationResult balancedBuyWithNeutralFiveException(
 219:             TradeSignal executionSignal, SignalDecision fiveMinute, SignalDecision oneHour, int entryQualityScore) {
 220:         ValidationResult balanced = balancedBuy(fiveMinute, oneHour);
 221:         if (balanced.allowed()) return balanced;
 222: 
 223:         boolean neutralFiveBullishOne = fiveMinute == SignalDecision.NEUTRAL && isBullish(oneHour);
 224:         BtcContextStatus btcStatus = executionSignal.getBtcContextStatus();
 225:         // FIX-112B: plain/strong BTC conflict cannot be rescued by this exception.
 226:         // FIX-141: nor can a failed freshness guard - STALE_CONTEXT is a real, evidenced
 227:         // block (missing/stale/future-dated/unknown-candle-time BTC context), not an
 228:         // "unavailable, non-authoritative" state. This exception must not restore the
 229:         // entry permission the freshness guard already withheld upstream.
 230:         // Other BTC states retain their existing upstream Production semantics.
 231:         boolean btcSafeForException = btcStatus != BtcContextStatus.CONFLICT
 232:                 && btcStatus != BtcContextStatus.STRONG_CONFLICT
 233:                 && btcStatus != BtcContextStatus.STALE_CONTEXT;
 234:         boolean strongEnough = executionSignal.getConfidenceScore() >= 72
 235:                 && entryQualityScore >= 70
 236:                 && btcSafeForException;
 237: 
 238:         if (neutralFiveBullishOne && strongEnough) {
 239:             return ValidationResult.allow(25, "BALANCED_NEUTRAL_5M",
 240:                     "BALANCED_NEUTRAL_5M: 25% exploratory entry approved because 5m is NEUTRAL "
 241:                              + "rather than bearish while 1h remains " + oneHour
 242:                             + ". 1m=" + executionSignal.getDecision()
 243:                             + ", confidence=" + executionSignal.getConfidenceScore()
 244:                              + ", 5m=NEUTRAL, 1h=" + oneHour
 245:                             + ", entryQuality=" + entryQualityScore
 246:                             + ", btcStatus=" + btcStatus
 247:                             + ", position=25%.");
 248:         }
 249: 
 250:         // FIX-11G / controlled Production behavior change:
 251:         // An already-approved fresh 1m BUY can appear before tactical 5m confirmation
 252:         // catches up. When 5m is still NEUTRAL and 1h is WATCH, neither timeframe is
 253:         // bearish, but the original BALANCED matrix has no authority for this transition.
 254:         // Grant only a 25% exploratory authority and only when FinalDecision already
 255:         // allowed entry, BTC has no hard conflict, and Entry Quality is at/above the
 256:         // existing chase cutoff. Do NOT add a second confidence threshold here: the
 257:         // upstream BUY + finalEntryAllowed decision already owns signal confidence.
 258:         //
 259:         // Golden rule: Replay = Production. Replay reaches this exact shared method
 260:         // through ExecutionReplayScope historical 5m/1h lookups; there is no Replay-only
 261:         // copy, threshold, or special execution behavior. validateBuyContext() remains
 262:         // intentionally unchanged so accumulated-evidence/recovery authority is not widened.
 263:         boolean neutralFiveWatchOne = fiveMinute == SignalDecision.NEUTRAL && oneHour == SignalDecision.WATCH;
 264:         if (neutralFiveWatchOne) {
 265:             boolean upstreamApproved = executionSignal.isFinalEntryAllowed();
 266:             boolean entryQualitySafe = entryQualityScore >= BALANCED_TRANSITIONAL_MIN_ENTRY_QUALITY;
 267:             // FIX-141: see btcSafeForException above - STALE_CONTEXT must not be rescued here either.
 268:             boolean btcSafeForTransitionalAuthority = btcStatus != BtcContextStatus.CONFLICT
 269:                     && btcStatus != BtcContextStatus.STRONG_CONFLICT
 270:                     && btcStatus != BtcContextStatus.STALE_CONTEXT;
 271: 
 272:             if (upstreamApproved && entryQualitySafe && btcSafeForTransitionalAuthority) {
 273:                 log.info("FIX-11G transitional BUY authority granted: signalId={}, symbol={}, generatedAt={}, "
 274:                                 + "decision={}, confidence={}, 5m={}, 1h={}, entryQuality={}, btcStatus={}, positionPercent=25",
 275:                         executionSignal.getId(), executionSignal.getSymbol(), executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(),
 276:                         executionSignal.getDecision(), executionSignal.getConfidenceScore(),
 277:                         fiveMinute, oneHour, entryQualityScore, btcStatus);
 278:                 return ValidationResult.allow(25, "BALANCED_NEUTRAL_5M_WATCH_1H",
 279:                         "BALANCED_NEUTRAL_5M_WATCH_1H: 25% exploratory entry approved for an already-approved "
 280:                                 + "1m BUY while 5m is NEUTRAL and 1h is WATCH. "
 281:                                 + "entryQuality=" + entryQualityScore
 282:                                 + ", btcStatus=" + btcStatus
 283:                                 + ", position=25%.");
 284:             }
 285: 
 286:             // INFO is intentional here: this exact transition is the new FIX-11G boundary.
 287:             // Keeping both grant and denial searchable by FIX-11G makes Production misses
 288:             // diagnosable from application logs without enabling global DEBUG logging.
 289:             log.info("FIX-11G transitional BUY authority not granted: signalId={}, symbol={}, generatedAt={}, "
 290:                             + "decision={}, confidence={}, finalEntryAllowed={}, 5m={}, 1h={}, entryQuality={}, "
 291:                             + "minEntryQuality={}, btcStatus={}",
 292:                     executionSignal.getId(), executionSignal.getSymbol(), executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(),
 293:                     executionSignal.getDecision(), executionSignal.getConfidenceScore(), upstreamApproved,
 294:                     fiveMinute, oneHour, entryQualityScore,
 295:                     BALANCED_TRANSITIONAL_MIN_ENTRY_QUALITY, btcStatus);
 296:         }
 297:         return balanced;
 298:     }
 299: 
 300:     private ValidationResult balancedBuy(SignalDecision five, SignalDecision one) {
 301: 
 302:         if (isBullish(five) && isBullish(one)) {
 303:             return ValidationResult.allow(100, "BALANCED_FULL",
 304:                     "Balanced profile approved 100%: both 5m and 1h are bullish.");
 305:         }
 306:         if (isBullish(five) && isWatchOrNeutral(one)) {
 307:             return ValidationResult.allow(75, "BALANCED_STRONG",
 308:                     "Balanced profile approved 75%: 5m is bullish while 1h is " + one + ".");
 309:         }
 310:         if (five == SignalDecision.WATCH && isBullish(one)) {
 311:             return ValidationResult.allow(75, "BALANCED_STRONG",
 312:                     "Balanced profile approved 75%: 1h is bullish while 5m is WATCH.");
 313:         }
 314:         if (five == SignalDecision.WATCH && one == SignalDecision.WATCH) {
 315:             return ValidationResult.allow(50, "BALANCED_EARLY",
 316:                     "Balanced profile approved 50%: both 5m and 1h are WATCH, so this is an early reduced entry.");
 317:         }
 318: 
 319:         return ValidationResult.reject("BALANCED_CONFIRMATION_INSUFFICIENT",
 320:                 "Balanced profile requires bullish/WATCH confirmation. Current 5m=" + five + ", 1h=" + one + ".");
 321:     }

 865:     private EntryQuality assessEntryQuality(TradeSignal current, BigDecimal authoritativeAtr, BigDecimal executionPrice) {
 866:         if (current == null || current.getGeneratedAt() == null || executionPrice == null
 867:                 || executionPrice.signum() <= 0) {
 868:             return new EntryQuality(0, "UNKNOWN", 0d, 0d, 0d, 0L, false, null, 0, 0d, false, null);
 869:         }
 870: 
 871:         Instant cutoff = current.getGeneratedAt().minus(EVIDENCE_WINDOW);
 872:         List<TradeSignal> recent = recentSignals(current.getSymbol(), EXECUTION_INTERVAL, current.getGeneratedAt());
 873: 
 874:         BigDecimal reference = executionPrice;
 875:         BigDecimal recentHigh = executionPrice;
 876:         Instant recentHighAt = current.getGeneratedAt();
 877:         for (TradeSignal s : recent) {
 878:             if (s.getGeneratedAt() == null || s.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(current.getGeneratedAt())) continue;
 879:             if (s.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(cutoff)) break;
 880:             if (s.getLatestPrice() != null && s.getLatestPrice().signum() > 0
 881:                     && s.getLatestPrice().compareTo(reference) < 0) {
 882:                 reference = s.getLatestPrice();
 883:             }
 884:             // FIX-091 / Fix 7: mirror the existing rolling-low memory with a rolling high.
 885:             // We intentionally use already-persisted signal prices so Replay and Production
 886:             // observe the same pre-wallet evidence and no new market-data dependency is added.
 887:             if (s.getLatestPrice() != null && s.getLatestPrice().signum() > 0
 888:                     && s.getLatestPrice().compareTo(recentHigh) > 0) {
 889:                 recentHigh = s.getLatestPrice();
 890:                 recentHighAt = s.getGeneratedAt();
 891:             }
 892:         }
 893: 
 894:         // FIX-055 + FIX-105: keep the opportunity anchor/best floor only while the opportunity
 895:         // is fresh. currentOpportunity() is Replay-aware, and age is measured exclusively from
 896:         // persisted/replayed candle-time evidence (startedAt -> signal.generatedAt), never wall-clock
 897:         // time. Therefore Production and Replay execute this exact same branch deterministically.
 898:         java.util.Optional<ExecutionOpportunity> activeOpportunity = currentOpportunity(
 899:                 current.getSymbol(), List.of("BUILDING", "WEAKENING", "BLOCKED", "CONFIRMED"));
 900:         String chaseReferenceSource = "ROLLING_30M";
 901:         if (activeOpportunity.isPresent()) {
 902:             ExecutionOpportunity opp = activeOpportunity.get();
 903:             boolean anchorStillFresh = false;
 904:             long opportunityAgeMinutesForReference = 0L;
 905: 
 906:             if (opp.getStartedAt() != null) {
 907:                 Duration opportunityAge = Duration.between(opp.getStartedAt(), current.getGeneratedAt());
 908:                 opportunityAgeMinutesForReference = Math.max(0L, opportunityAge.toMinutes());
 909:                 // FIX-105 boundary is inclusive: exactly 120 minutes keeps FIX-055 protection;
 910:                 // the first instant beyond two hours returns authority to the rolling 30m reference.
 911:                 anchorStillFresh = !opportunityAge.isNegative()
 912:                         && opportunityAge.compareTo(ANCHOR_MAX_AGE) <= 0;
 913:             }
 914: 
 915:             if (anchorStillFresh) {
 916:                 BigDecimal anchor = opp.getAnchorEntryPrice();
 917:                 BigDecimal best = opp.getBestEntryPrice();
 918:                 if (anchor != null && anchor.signum() > 0 && anchor.compareTo(reference) < 0) {
 919:                     reference = anchor;
 920:                     chaseReferenceSource = "OPPORTUNITY_ANCHOR";
 921:                 }
 922:                 if (best != null && best.signum() > 0 && best.compareTo(reference) < 0) {
 923:                     reference = best;
 924:                     chaseReferenceSource = "OPPORTUNITY_BEST";
 925:                 }
 926:             }
 927: 
 928:             // FIX-105 diagnostics are deliberately log-only in v1: no schema expansion is required
 929:             // to prove which reference won in Production or Replay. Persisted decision/opportunity
 930:             // evidence remains unchanged.
 931:             log.debug("FIX-105 chase reference symbol={} source={} reference={} opportunityAgeMinutes={} anchorFresh={}",
 932:                     current.getSymbol(), chaseReferenceSource, reference, opportunityAgeMinutesForReference, anchorStillFresh);
 933:         }
 934: 
 935:         double expansionPercent = executionPrice.subtract(reference)
 936:                 .divide(reference, 8, RoundingMode.HALF_UP)
 937:                 .multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(100)).doubleValue();
 938: 
 939:         double atrExtension = 0d;
 940:         BigDecimal atrForExtension = authoritativeAtr != null && authoritativeAtr.signum() > 0
 941:                 ? authoritativeAtr
 942:                 : current.getAtrAtSignal();
 943:         if (atrForExtension != null && atrForExtension.signum() > 0) {
 944:             atrExtension = executionPrice.subtract(reference).max(BigDecimal.ZERO)
 945:                     .divide(atrForExtension, 8, RoundingMode.HALF_UP).doubleValue();
 946:         }
 947: 
 948:         long ageMinutes = activeOpportunity
 949:                 .map(o -> o.getStartedAt() == null ? 0L
 950:                         : Math.max(0L, Duration.between(o.getStartedAt(), current.getGeneratedAt()).toMinutes()))
 951:                 .orElse(0L);
 952: 
 953:         double rr = currentRewardRisk(current, executionPrice);
 954: 
 955:         // FIX-091 / Fix 7: a prior high is considered a recent rejection only when price
 956:         // subsequently backed away and the current entry is returning close to that same zone.
 957:         // v1 is deliberately conservative: this only reduces Entry Quality; it never hard-vetoes.
 958:         int rejectionCount = 0;
 959:         double distanceFromRejectedHighPercent = 0d;
 960:         BigDecimal recentRejectedHigh = null;
 961:         if (recentHigh != null && recentHigh.signum() > 0 && recentHighAt != null
 962:                 && recentHighAt.isBefore(current.getGeneratedAt())) {
 963:             for (TradeSignal s : recent) {
 964:                 if (s.getGeneratedAt() == null || !s.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(recentHighAt)
 965:                         || s.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(current.getGeneratedAt()) || s.getLatestPrice() == null) continue;
 966:                 if (s.getLatestPrice().compareTo(recentHigh) < 0) rejectionCount++;
 967:             }
 968:             distanceFromRejectedHighPercent = recentHigh.subtract(executionPrice).abs()
 969:                     .divide(recentHigh, 8, RoundingMode.HALF_UP)
 970:                     .multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(100)).doubleValue();
 971:             if (rejectionCount >= 2 && distanceFromRejectedHighPercent <= 0.50d) {
 972:                 recentRejectedHigh = recentHigh;
 973:             }
 974:         }
 975: 
 976:         int score = 100;
 977: 
 978:         if (expansionPercent > 8d) score -= 50;
 979:         else if (expansionPercent > 5d) score -= 35;
 980:         else if (expansionPercent > 3d) score -= 22;
 981:         else if (expansionPercent > 1.5d) score -= 10;
 982: 
 983:         if (atrExtension > 6d) score -= 30;
 984:         else if (atrExtension > 4d) score -= 20;
 985:         else if (atrExtension > 2.5d) score -= 10;
 986: 
 987:         if (ageMinutes > 60) score -= 15;
 988:         else if (ageMinutes > 40) score -= 10;
 989:         else if (ageMinutes > 20) score -= 5;
 990: 
 991:         if (rr <= 0d || rr < 1d) score -= 30;
 992:         else if (rr < 1.25d) score -= 15;
 993:         else if (rr >= 2d) score += 5;
 994: 
 995:         if (recentRejectedHigh != null) {
 996:             // FIX-091 / Fix 7 v1 is penalty/size-reduction only. A rejection penalty must not
 997:             // by itself push an otherwise non-chase entry below the hard CHASE_ENTRY cutoff.
 998:             int beforeRejectionPenalty = score;
 999:             score -= RECENT_REJECTION_ENTRY_QUALITY_PENALTY;
1000:             if (beforeRejectionPenalty >= CHASE_ENTRY_CUTOFF) {
1001:                 score = Math.max(CHASE_ENTRY_CUTOFF, score);
1002:             }
1003:         }
1004: 
1005:         if ("HIGH_VOLATILITY".equals(String.valueOf(current.getMarketRegime()))) score -= 10;
1006: 
1007:         // FIX-055: STOP_EXPOSED remains a soft warning (TARGET_BLOCKED is still the
1008:         // hard liquidity veto), but an exposed stop must reduce entry quality and
1009:         // therefore position size or block a chase through the existing quality guard.
1010:         if (current.getLiquidityStatus() == LiquidityContextStatus.STOP_EXPOSED) {
1011:             score -= STOP_EXPOSED_ENTRY_QUALITY_PENALTY;
1012:         }
1013: 
1014:         if ("BREAKOUT".equals(String.valueOf(current.getMarketRegime()))
1015:                 && expansionPercent <= 3d && atrExtension <= 2.5d) score += 5;
1016: 
1017:         score = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, score));
1018:         String classification = score >= 85 ? "EXCELLENT_ENTRY"
1019:                 : score >= 70 ? "GOOD_ENTRY"
1020:                 : score >= 55 ? "ACCEPTABLE_ENTRY"
1021:                 : score >= CHASE_ENTRY_CUTOFF ? "LATE_ENTRY"
1022:                 : "CHASE_ENTRY";
1023: 
1024:         boolean moderateBtcConflict = current.getBtcContextStatus() == com.crypto.domain.BtcContextStatus.CONFLICT;
1025:         boolean transitionProbe = "TRANSITION_PROBE".equals(current.getEntryAuthority());
1026:         Integer authorityCap = current.getEntryAuthorityMaxPositionPercent();
1027:         return new EntryQuality(score, classification, expansionPercent, atrExtension, rr, ageMinutes,
1028:                 moderateBtcConflict, recentRejectedHigh, rejectionCount, distanceFromRejectedHighPercent,
1029:                 transitionProbe, authorityCap);

1145:     /**
1146:      * FIX-056: final execution-time revalidation. The signal decision remains immutable,
1147:      * but a wallet BUY must be re-checked against the current canonical market price before
1148:      * sizing/fill. This prevents a valid old signal from becoming a late chase by execution time.
1149:      */
1150:     public ExecutionDecision revalidateAtExecutionPrice(TradeSignal signal, ExecutionDecision approvedDecision, BigDecimal executionPrice) {
1151:         // FIX-122: recheck only stamped initial approvals; ADD approvals carry no stamp.
1152:         if (stopLossEvidence != null) approvedDecision = stopLossEvidence.recheck(signal, approvedDecision);
1153:         if (approvedDecision != null && !approvedDecision.allowed()) return approvedDecision;
1154:         if (signal == null || approvedDecision == null || !approvedDecision.allowed()
1155:                 || executionPrice == null || executionPrice.signum() <= 0) {
1156:             return ExecutionDecision.reject("EXECUTION_PRICE_INVALID",
1157:                     "A fresh positive execution price is required before wallet execution.");
1158:         }
1159:         if (signal.getStopLoss() != null && executionPrice.compareTo(signal.getStopLoss()) <= 0) {
1160:             return ExecutionDecision.reject("EXECUTION_PRICE_AT_OR_BELOW_STOP",
1161:                     "Current execution price is already at/below the signal stop; BUY cancelled.", approvedDecision.evidence());
1162:         }
1163:         if (signal.getTakeProfit() != null && executionPrice.compareTo(signal.getTakeProfit()) >= 0) {
1164:             return ExecutionDecision.reject("EXECUTION_PRICE_AT_OR_ABOVE_TARGET",
1165:                     "Current execution price has already reached/exceeded the signal target; BUY cancelled.", approvedDecision.evidence());
1166:         }
1167:         EntryQuality quality = assessEntryQualityAtPrice(signal, executionPrice);
1168:         return applyInitialEntryQualityGuard(approvedDecision, quality).withStopBoundary(approvedDecision.stopBoundary());
1169:     }

1171:     private ExecutionDecision applyInitialEntryQualityGuard(ExecutionDecision decision, EntryQuality q) {
1172:         if (decision == null || !decision.allowed()) return decision;
1173:         if (q.score() < CHASE_ENTRY_CUTOFF) {
1174:             return ExecutionDecision.building(
1175:                     "CHASE_ENTRY_BLOCKED",
1176:                     "The market signal is valid, but Entry Quality is only " + q.score()
1177:                             + "/100 (" + q.classification() + "). Price has already expanded "
1178:                             + format(q.expansionPercent()) + "% from the recent opportunity base and "
1179:                             + format(q.atrExtension()) + " ATR. The engine will not buy a late-stage chase.",
1180:                     decision.evidence());
1181:         }
1182: 
1183:         int cap = q.score() >= 85 ? 50 : q.score() >= 70 ? 40 : 25;
1184:         StringBuilder capReason = new StringBuilder(" Entry Quality ")
1185:                 .append(q.score()).append("/100 (").append(q.classification()).append(")");
1186: 
1187:         // FIX-091 / Fix 1: moderate BTC conflict stays fast and BUY-eligible, but may not
1188:         // receive full exposure. Classification remains owned by BtcMarketContextService.
1189:         if (q.moderateBtcConflict()) {
1190:             int btcConflictCap = q.score() >= 85 ? BTC_MODERATE_CONFLICT_EXCELLENT_CAP
1191:                     : q.score() >= 70 ? BTC_MODERATE_CONFLICT_GOOD_CAP
1192:                     : BTC_MODERATE_CONFLICT_LATE_CAP;
1193:             cap = Math.min(cap, btcConflictCap);
1194:             capReason.append("; BTC context is CONFLICT, so the same tiered initial-entry model reduces exposure to ")
1195:                     .append(btcConflictCap).append("%");
1196:         }
1197:         // FIX-091 / Fix 5: EntryAuthority is only a maximum size. The current signal has already
1198:         // travelled through the normal direct-candidate + validateBuy flow before this guard runs.
1199:         // The EntryQuality snapshot is created from the current TradeSignal, so the explicit cap is
1200:         // carried through the signal fields without inventing a parallel execution route.

1807:     private double currentRewardRisk(TradeSignal signal) {

1811:     private double currentRewardRisk(TradeSignal signal, BigDecimal marketPrice) {
1812:         if (signal == null || marketPrice == null || signal.getStopLoss() == null || signal.getTakeProfit() == null) return 0d;
1813:         java.math.BigDecimal risk = marketPrice.subtract(signal.getStopLoss()).abs();
1814:         java.math.BigDecimal reward = signal.getTakeProfit().subtract(marketPrice);
1815:         if (risk.signum() <= 0 || reward.signum() <= 0) return 0d;
1816:         return reward.divide(risk, 8, java.math.RoundingMode.HALF_UP).doubleValue();
1817:     }

2214:     private boolean isSupportiveCurrentSignal(TradeSignal signal) {

2221:     private HardRiskBlock nonAtrHardRiskBlock(TradeSignal signal) {
2222:         if (!signal.isStrategyEntryAllowed()) {
2223:             return HardRiskBlock.block("STRATEGY_ENTRY_BLOCKED", "Selected market strategy does not allow entry.");
2224:         }
2225:         if (!signal.isBtcContextEntryAllowed()) {
2226:             return HardRiskBlock.block("BTC_CONTEXT_BLOCKED", "BTC context issued an entry veto.");
2227:         }
2228:         if (!signal.isDerivativesEntryAllowed()) {
2229:             return HardRiskBlock.block("DERIVATIVES_BLOCKED", "Funding/open-interest positioning issued an entry veto.");
2230:         }
2231:         // With wall-lifecycle intelligence, TARGET_BLOCKED should only remain false-entry-allowed
2232:         // when the wall is genuinely strong/relevant. Weakening walls no longer reach this hard veto.
2233:         if (!signal.isLiquidityEntryAllowed()
2234:                 && signal.getLiquidityStatus() == LiquidityContextStatus.TARGET_BLOCKED) {
2235:             return HardRiskBlock.block("STRONG_TARGET_WALL",
2236:                     "A strong nearby target-side wall is still active; execution remains blocked until fresh order-book evidence changes.");
2237:         }
2238:         return HardRiskBlock.none();

2241:     private Evidence evidence(TradeSignal current) {
2242:         // FIX-122: initial-entry scope only; additions keep their original evidence.
2243:         if (stopLossEvidence != null) stopLossEvidence.requireCurrent(current);
2244:         Instant cutoff = current.getGeneratedAt().minus(EVIDENCE_WINDOW);

2256:             if (signal.getGeneratedAt() == null || signal.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(current.getGeneratedAt())) continue;
2257:             if (signal.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(cutoff)) break;

2259:

2261:                 oldestObservedAt = signal.getGeneratedAt();
2262:             }
2263:

2265:

2267:             SignalDecision original = signal.getOriginalDecision();
2268:             SignalDecision effective = strongerDecision(decision, original);
2269: 
2270:             if (effective == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL) {
2271:                 bearish++;
2272:                 health += scaledHealthContribution(HEALTH_1M_STRONG_SELL, signal);
2273:                 if (lastBearishAt == null) lastBearishAt = signal.getGeneratedAt();
2274:                 break; // a strong bearish 1m event is still a true memory boundary
2275:             }
2276: 
2277:             if (effective == SignalDecision.SELL) {
2278:                 bearish++;
2279:                 evidenceScore -= WEAK_SELL_EVIDENCE_PENALTY;
2280:                 health += scaledHealthContribution(HEALTH_1M_SELL, signal);
2281:                 if (lastBearishAt == null) lastBearishAt = signal.getGeneratedAt();
2282:                 continue;
2283:             }
2284: 
2285:             if (isBullish(effective)) {
2286:                 buy++;
2287:                 evidenceScore += 3;
2288:                 health += scaledHealthContribution(HEALTH_1M_BUY, signal);
2289:                 if (signal.getId() != null) {
2290:                     signalIds.add(signal.getId());
2291:                 }
2292:             } else if (decision == SignalDecision.WATCH
2293:                     && signal.getTotalScore() >= WATCH_EVIDENCE_MIN_SCORE
2294:                     && signal.getConfidenceScore() >= WATCH_EVIDENCE_MIN_CONFIDENCE) {
2295:                 watch++;
2296:                 evidenceScore += 1;
2297:                 health += scaledHealthContribution(HEALTH_1M_WATCH, signal);
2298:                 if (signal.getId() != null) {
2299:                     signalIds.add(signal.getId());
2300:                 }
2301:             } else if (decision == SignalDecision.NEUTRAL) {
2302:                 neutral++;
2303:                 health += HEALTH_1M_NEUTRAL;
2304:             }
2305: 
2306:             if (decision == SignalDecision.WATCH || isBullish(decision)) {
2307:                 scoreTotal += signal.getTotalScore();
2308:                 confidenceTotal += signal.getConfidenceScore();
2309:                 qualityCount++;
2310:             }
2311:         }
2312: 
2313:         int evidenceMomentum = calculateEvidenceMomentum(observedSequence);
2314:         health += evidenceMomentum;
2315: 
2316:         TradeSignal five = latestAtOrBefore(current, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL, FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE);
2317:         TradeSignal one = latestAtOrBefore(current, TREND_INTERVAL, ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE);
2318: 
2319:         // Higher timeframe evidence actively restores or weakens opportunity health.
2320:         // This is deliberately generic: no symbol-specific values are used.
2321:         health += timeframeHealthContribution(five, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL);
2322:         health += timeframeHealthContribution(one, TREND_INTERVAL);
2323:

2325:                 : Math.max(0, Duration.between(oldestObservedAt, current.getGeneratedAt()).toMinutes());

2327:         health -= Math.min(6, (int) (ageMinutes / 10));

2331:         int previousHealth = currentOpportunity(current.getSymbol(), List.of("BUILDING", "WEAKENING", "BLOCKED", "CONFIRMED"))
2332:                 // FIX-122: do not compare fresh health to a pre-stop opportunity snapshot.
2333:                 .filter(o -> stopLossEvidence == null || stopLossEvidence.eligible(o.getLatestSignal(), "PREVIOUS_HEALTH"))
2334:                 .map(ExecutionOpportunity::getOpportunityHealth)
2335:                 .orElse(OPPORTUNITY_HEALTH_START);
2336:         int healthMomentum = health - previousHealth;
2337: 
2338:         return new Evidence(
2339:                 buy + watch + neutral + bearish,
2340:                 buy,
2341:                 watch,
2342:                 neutral,
2343:                 bearish,
2344:                 evidenceScore,
2345:                 health,
2346:                 healthMomentum,
2347:                 evidenceMomentum,
2348:                 qualityCount == 0 ? 0 : Math.round((float) scoreTotal / qualityCount),
2349:                 qualityCount == 0 ? 0 : Math.round((float) confidenceTotal / qualityCount),
2350:                 five == null ? null : five.getDecision(),
2351:                 one == null ? null : one.getDecision(),
2352:                 lastBearishAt,
2353:                 signalIds.stream().filter(Objects::nonNull).toList()
2354:         );
2355:     }
2356: 
2357:     private int calculateEvidenceMomentum(List<TradeSignal> observedDescending) {
2358:         if (observedDescending == null || observedDescending.size() < 2) return 0;
2359: 
2360:         List<TradeSignal> sequence = new ArrayList<>(observedDescending);
2361:         Collections.reverse(sequence); // oldest -> newest
2362:         if (sequence.size() > EVIDENCE_MOMENTUM_WINDOW) {
2363:             sequence = new ArrayList<>(sequence.subList(sequence.size() - EVIDENCE_MOMENTUM_WINDOW, sequence.size()));
2364:         }
2365: 
2366:         int momentum = 0;
2367:         for (int i = 1; i < sequence.size(); i++) {
2368:             TradeSignal previous = sequence.get(i - 1);
2369:             TradeSignal current = sequence.get(i);
2370:             int previousStrength = decisionStrength(strongerDecision(previous.getDecision(), previous.getOriginalDecision()));
2371:             int currentStrength = decisionStrength(strongerDecision(current.getDecision(), current.getOriginalDecision()));
2372:             int delta = currentStrength - previousStrength;
2373:             int recencyWeight = Math.min(3, i);
2374:             momentum += delta * 3 * recencyWeight;
2375:         }
2376: 
2377:         TradeSignal latest = sequence.get(sequence.size() - 1);
2378:         int latestStrength = decisionStrength(strongerDecision(latest.getDecision(), latest.getOriginalDecision()));
2379:         momentum += latestStrength * 2;
2380:         return Math.max(-EVIDENCE_MOMENTUM_CAP, Math.min(EVIDENCE_MOMENTUM_CAP, momentum));
2381:     }
2382: 
2383:     private int decisionStrength(SignalDecision decision) {
2384:         if (decision == null) return 0;
2385:         return switch (decision) {
2386:             case STRONG_BUY -> 2;
2387:             case BUY, WATCH -> 1;
2388:             case NEUTRAL -> 0;
2389:             case SELL -> -1;
2390:             case STRONG_SELL -> -2;
2391:         };
2392:     }
2393: 
2394:     private int timeframeHealthContribution(TradeSignal signal, String interval) {
2395:         if (signal == null) return 0;
2396:         SignalDecision decision = strongerDecision(signal.getDecision(), signal.getOriginalDecision());
2397:         int base;
2398:         if (CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL.equals(interval)) {
2399:             base = switch (decision) {
2400:                 case BUY, STRONG_BUY -> HEALTH_5M_BUY;
2401:                 case WATCH -> HEALTH_5M_WATCH;
2402:                 case SELL -> HEALTH_5M_SELL;
2403:                 case STRONG_SELL -> HEALTH_5M_STRONG_SELL;
2404:                 default -> 0;
2405:             };
2406:         } else {
2407:             base = switch (decision) {
2408:                 case BUY, STRONG_BUY -> HEALTH_1H_BUY;
2409:                 case WATCH -> HEALTH_1H_WATCH;
2410:                 case SELL -> HEALTH_1H_SELL;
2411:                 case STRONG_SELL -> HEALTH_1H_STRONG_SELL;
2412:                 default -> 0;
2413:             };
2414:         }
2415:         return scaledHealthContribution(base, signal);
2416:     }
2417: 
2418:     private int scaledHealthContribution(int base, TradeSignal signal) {
2419:         if (base == 0 || signal == null) return 0;
2420:         int quality = Math.round((signal.getTotalScore() + signal.getConfidenceScore()) / 2.0f);
2421:         double factor = quality >= 80 ? 1.25 : quality >= 70 ? 1.0 : quality >= 60 ? 0.8 : 0.6;
2422:         return (int) Math.round(base * factor);
2423:     }

2426:      * Downstream execution must respect FinalDecisionService as the authority.
2427:      * originalDecision is retained for audit/explainability only and is used
2428:      * solely as a null fallback for legacy rows.
2429:      */
2430:     private SignalDecision strongerDecision(SignalDecision decision, SignalDecision original) {
2431:         return decision != null ? decision : original;

 865:     private EntryQuality assessEntryQuality(TradeSignal current, BigDecimal authoritativeAtr, BigDecimal executionPrice) {
 866:         if (current == null || current.getGeneratedAt() == null || executionPrice == null
 867:                 || executionPrice.signum() <= 0) {

 875:         BigDecimal recentHigh = executionPrice;
 876:         Instant recentHighAt = current.getGeneratedAt();

 878:             if (s.getGeneratedAt() == null || s.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(current.getGeneratedAt())) continue;

 883:             }

 886:             // observe the same pre-wallet evidence and no new market-data dependency is added.
 887:             if (s.getLatestPrice() != null && s.getLatestPrice().signum() > 0

 898:         java.util.Optional<ExecutionOpportunity> activeOpportunity = currentOpportunity(

 901:         if (activeOpportunity.isPresent()) {

 910:                 // the first instant beyond two hours returns authority to the rolling 30m reference.
 911:                 anchorStillFresh = !opportunityAge.isNegative()
 912:                         && opportunityAge.compareTo(ANCHOR_MAX_AGE) <= 0;
 913:             }
 914: 
 915:             if (anchorStillFresh) {
 916:                 BigDecimal anchor = opp.getAnchorEntryPrice();
 917:                 BigDecimal best = opp.getBestEntryPrice();
 918:                 if (anchor != null && anchor.signum() > 0 && anchor.compareTo(reference) < 0) {
 919:                     reference = anchor;
 920:                     chaseReferenceSource = "OPPORTUNITY_ANCHOR";

 923:                     reference = best;
 924:                     chaseReferenceSource = "OPPORTUNITY_BEST";
 925:                 }

 930:             // evidence remains unchanged.

 933:         }
 934:

 957:         // v1 is deliberately conservative: this only reduces Entry Quality; it never hard-vetoes.
 958:         int rejectionCount = 0;

 979:         else if (expansionPercent > 5d) score -= 35;
 980:         else if (expansionPercent > 3d) score -= 22;
 981:         else if (expansionPercent > 1.5d) score -= 10;
 982:

 985:         else if (atrExtension > 2.5d) score -= 10;

 989:         else if (ageMinutes > 20) score -= 5;
 990:

 994:

1020:                 : score >= 55 ? "ACCEPTABLE_ENTRY"

1026:         Integer authorityCap = current.getEntryAuthorityMaxPositionPercent();

1028:                 moderateBtcConflict, recentRejectedHigh, rejectionCount, distanceFromRejectedHighPercent,

1150:     public ExecutionDecision revalidateAtExecutionPrice(TradeSignal signal, ExecutionDecision approvedDecision, BigDecimal executionPrice) {
1151:         // FIX-122: recheck only stamped initial approvals; ADD approvals carry no stamp.
1152:         if (stopLossEvidence != null) approvedDecision = stopLossEvidence.recheck(signal, approvedDecision);
1153:         if (approvedDecision != null && !approvedDecision.allowed()) return approvedDecision;

1165:                     "Current execution price has already reached/exceeded the signal target; BUY cancelled.", approvedDecision.evidence());

1171:     private ExecutionDecision applyInitialEntryQualityGuard(ExecutionDecision decision, EntryQuality q) {
1172:         if (decision == null || !decision.allowed()) return decision;
1173:         if (q.score() < CHASE_ENTRY_CUTOFF) {
1174:             return ExecutionDecision.building(
1175:                     "CHASE_ENTRY_BLOCKED",
1176:                     "The market signal is valid, but Entry Quality is only " + q.score()
1177:                             + "/100 (" + q.classification() + "). Price has already expanded "
1178:                             + format(q.expansionPercent()) + "% from the recent opportunity base and "
1179:                             + format(q.atrExtension()) + " ATR. The engine will not buy a late-stage chase.",
1180:                     decision.evidence());
1181:         }
1182: 
1183:         int cap = q.score() >= 85 ? 50 : q.score() >= 70 ? 40 : 25;
1184:         StringBuilder capReason = new StringBuilder(" Entry Quality ")
1185:                 .append(q.score()).append("/100 (").append(q.classification()).append(")");
1186: 
1187:         // FIX-091 / Fix 1: moderate BTC conflict stays fast and BUY-eligible, but may not
1188:         // receive full exposure. Classification remains owned by BtcMarketContextService.
1189:         if (q.moderateBtcConflict()) {
1190:             int btcConflictCap = q.score() >= 85 ? BTC_MODERATE_CONFLICT_EXCELLENT_CAP
1191:                     : q.score() >= 70 ? BTC_MODERATE_CONFLICT_GOOD_CAP
1192:                     : BTC_MODERATE_CONFLICT_LATE_CAP;
1193:             cap = Math.min(cap, btcConflictCap);
1194:             capReason.append("; BTC context is CONFLICT, so the same tiered initial-entry model reduces exposure to ")
1195:                     .append(btcConflictCap).append("%");
1196:         }
1197:         // FIX-091 / Fix 5: EntryAuthority is only a maximum size. The current signal has already
1198:         // travelled through the normal direct-candidate + validateBuy flow before this guard runs.
1199:         // The EntryQuality snapshot is created from the current TradeSignal, so the explicit cap is
1200:         // carried through the signal fields without inventing a parallel execution route.
1201:         if (q.entryAuthorityMaxPositionPercent() != null && q.entryAuthorityMaxPositionPercent() > 0) {
1202:             cap = Math.min(cap, q.entryAuthorityMaxPositionPercent());
1203:             if (q.transitionProbe()) {
1204:                 capReason.append("; BREAKOUT_TRANSITION probe authority caps exposure at ")
1205:                         .append(q.entryAuthorityMaxPositionPercent()).append("%");
1206:             }
1207:         }
1208:         if (q.recentRejectedHigh() != null) {
1209:             capReason.append("; recent rejection zone near ").append(q.recentRejectedHigh())
1210:                     .append(" reduced entry quality without creating a hard veto");
1211:         }
1212: 
1213:         int reduced = Math.min(decision.positionPercent(), cap);
1214:         if (reduced == decision.positionPercent()) return decision;
1215: 
1216:         return ExecutionDecision.allow(
1217:                 decision.source(),
1218:                 decision.code(),
1219:                 reduced,
1220:                 decision.explanation() + capReason + " capped the initial allocation at " + reduced
1221:                         + "% so confirmation can add later instead of committing full size at once.",
1222:                 decision.evidence());
1223:     }

1225:     /**
1226:      * FIX-119 ƒ?" Accumulated-evidence late-entry timing deferral.
1227:      *
1228:      * ACCUMULATED_EVIDENCE is opportunity memory. It may legitimately open a
1229:      * position without a current BUY when persistent WATCH evidence has matured;
1230:      * ICPUSDT #1702 is a proven example and must remain executable.
1231:      *
1232:      * The defect appears when that same WATCH-only authority reaches a price that
1233:      * Entry Quality already classifies as LATE_ENTRY. In the current implementation,
1234:      * scores 50-54 remain executable with reduced sizing, including cases where a
1235:      * recent rejection-zone penalty was deliberately floored at the 50-point chase
1236:      * cutoff. Historical BTCUSDT #1701, EDUUSDT #1716 and UNIUSDT #1731 demonstrate
1237:      * that this can convert a valid opportunity into a poorly timed initial entry.
1238:      *
1239:      * FIX-119 therefore defers only when all of the following are simultaneously true:
1240:      *   - the candidate is an allowed ACCUMULATED_EVIDENCE initial entry;
1241:      *   - Entry Quality is LATE_ENTRY;
1242:      *   - accumulated fresh 1m evidence contains zero BUY observations; and
1243:      *   - fresh 5m setup context is not BUY/STRONG_BUY.
1244:      *
1245:      * The result stays BUILDING rather than being rejected. Existing opportunity
1246:      * evidence is preserved by saveOpportunity(), so subsequent signals can release
1247:      * the opportunity when price geometry improves or fresh BUY authority appears.
1248:      *
1249:      * Good controls intentionally preserved:
1250:      *   - ICPUSDT #1702: 0 BUY / 10 WATCH but ACCEPTABLE_ENTRY 55 -> unchanged.
1251:      *   - SUIUSDT #1696: LATE_ENTRY 50 but accumulated BUY evidence and 5m BUY
1252:      *     -> unchanged.
1253:      *
1254:      * A 1h BUY by itself does not bypass this guard. EDUUSDT #1716 had 1h BUY /
1255:      * BALANCED_STRONG but remained a badly timed WATCH-only accumulated entry.

2078:     private ExecutionDecision balancedEarlyPostBearishGuard(
2079:             TradeSignal current, Evidence evidence, EntryQuality entryQuality,
2080:             TradeExecutionValidationService.ValidationResult validation) {
2081:         if (validation == null || !validation.allowed() || !"BALANCED_EARLY".equals(validation.code())) return null;
2082:         if (current == null || current.getGeneratedAt() == null) return null;
2083: 
2084:         List<TradeSignal> fiveMinuteHistory = recentSignals(current.getSymbol(), CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL, current.getGeneratedAt()).stream()
2085:                 .filter(s -> s != null && s.getGeneratedAt() != null && !s.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(current.getGeneratedAt()))
2086:                 .sorted(java.util.Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt).reversed())
2087:                 .toList();
2088: 
2089:         Instant cutoff = current.getGeneratedAt().minus(BALANCED_EARLY_BEARISH_LOOKBACK);
2090:         TradeSignal lastBearishFive = fiveMinuteHistory.stream()
2091:                 .filter(s -> !s.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(cutoff))
2092:                 .filter(s -> isBearish(strongerDecision(s.getDecision(), s.getOriginalDecision())))
2093:                 .findFirst()
2094:                 .orElse(null);
2095:         if (lastBearishFive == null) return null; // existing BALANCED_EARLY behavior stays untouched
2096: 
2097:         int consecutiveRecoveredFive = 0;
2098:         for (TradeSignal five : fiveMinuteHistory) {
2099:             if (!five.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(lastBearishFive.getGeneratedAt())) break;
2100:             SignalDecision decision = strongerDecision(five.getDecision(), five.getOriginalDecision());
2101:             if (decision == SignalDecision.WATCH || isBullish(decision)) consecutiveRecoveredFive++;
2102:             else break;
2103:         }
2104: 
2105:         boolean healthOk = evidence.opportunityHealth() >= BALANCED_EARLY_POST_BEARISH_MIN_HEALTH;
2106:         boolean evidenceOk = evidence.evidenceScore() >= BALANCED_EARLY_POST_BEARISH_MIN_EVIDENCE;
2107:         boolean recoveryOk = consecutiveRecoveredFive >= BALANCED_EARLY_POST_BEARISH_REQUIRED_5M_RECOVERY;
2108:         boolean qualityOk = entryQuality != null && entryQuality.score() >= BALANCED_EARLY_POST_BEARISH_MIN_ENTRY_QUALITY;
2109: 
2110:         if (!healthOk || !evidenceOk || !recoveryOk || !qualityOk) {
2111:             return ExecutionDecision.building(
2112:                     "BALANCED_EARLY_POST_BEARISH_RECOVERY_REQUIRED",
2113:                     "BALANCED_EARLY is paused after a fresh 5m bearish confirmation. "
2114:                             + "Require health>=" + BALANCED_EARLY_POST_BEARISH_MIN_HEALTH
2115:                             + ", evidence>=" + BALANCED_EARLY_POST_BEARISH_MIN_EVIDENCE
2116:                             + ", " + BALANCED_EARLY_POST_BEARISH_REQUIRED_5M_RECOVERY
2117:                             + " consecutive recovered 5m WATCH/BUY states, and Entry Quality>="
2118:                             + BALANCED_EARLY_POST_BEARISH_MIN_ENTRY_QUALITY
2119:                             + ". Actual health=" + evidence.opportunityHealth()
2120:                             + ", evidence=" + evidence.evidenceScore()
2121:                             + ", recovered5m=" + consecutiveRecoveredFive
2122:                             + ", entryQuality=" + (entryQuality == null ? 0 : entryQuality.score()) + ".",
2123:                     evidence);
2124:         }
2125: 
2126:         int reduced = Math.min(validation.positionPercent(), BALANCED_EARLY_POST_BEARISH_MAX_POSITION);
2127:         return ExecutionDecision.allow(
2128:                 "IMMEDIATE_VALIDATION",
2129:                 "BALANCED_EARLY_POST_BEARISH_RECOVERED",
2130:                 reduced,
2131:                 "BALANCED_EARLY recovered safely after recent 5m bearish context: health="
2132:                         + evidence.opportunityHealth() + ", evidence=" + evidence.evidenceScore()
2133:                         + ", recovered5m=" + consecutiveRecoveredFive + ", Entry Quality="
2134:                         + entryQuality.score() + ". Initial exposure capped at " + reduced
2135:                         + "% so normal progressive confirmation can add later.",
2136:                 evidence);
2137:     }

2146:     /**
2147:      * FIX-064 / ENA #703, PEPE #756, SUI #802:
2148:      * Derived BUY/WATCH fields can lag an exhausted or rejected price impulse. Before the three
2149:      * affected entry routes (SCOUT, DEFERRED_CONTINUATION and ACCUMULATED_EVIDENCE) open fresh
2150:      * risk, inspect only candles that were fully CLOSED at signal.generatedAt. This deliberately
2151:      * does not alter market-regime categories, scores, BUY thresholds or accumulated-evidence
2152:      * semantics. Missing candle/ATR data fails open so data gaps cannot silently disable proven
2153:      * winning routes. Production and Replay use the same timestamp-bounded repository query.
2154:      */
2155:     private ImmediatePriceAction immediatePriceAction(TradeSignal signal) {
2156:         if (signal == null || signal.getGeneratedAt() == null || signal.getAtrAtSignal() == null
2157:                 || signal.getAtrAtSignal().signum() <= 0) {
2158:             return ImmediatePriceAction.supportive("raw candle/ATR unavailable; existing behavior preserved");
2159:         }
2160: 
2161:         List<Candle> candles = candleRepository.findClosedCandlesClosedAtOrBefore(
2162:                 signal.getSymbol(), EXECUTION_INTERVAL, signal.getGeneratedAt(),
2163:                 PageRequest.of(0, IMMEDIATE_PRICE_ACTION_LOOKBACK_CANDLES));
2164:         if (candles == null || candles.size() < 2) {
2165:             return ImmediatePriceAction.supportive("insufficient closed-candle history; existing behavior preserved");
2166:         }
2167: 
2168:         Candle latest = candles.get(0);
2169:         Candle previous = candles.get(1);
2170:         if (latest.getOpenPrice() == null || latest.getHighPrice() == null || latest.getLowPrice() == null
2171:                 || latest.getClosePrice() == null || previous.getClosePrice() == null) {
2172:             return ImmediatePriceAction.supportive("incomplete closed-candle OHLC; existing behavior preserved");
2173:         }
2174: 
2175:         BigDecimal atr = signal.getAtrAtSignal();
2176:         BigDecimal range = latest.getHighPrice().subtract(latest.getLowPrice()).abs();
2177:         double closeLocation = range.signum() == 0 ? 0.5d
2178:                 : latest.getClosePrice().subtract(latest.getLowPrice())
2179:                         .divide(range, 12, RoundingMode.HALF_UP).doubleValue();
2180:         closeLocation = Math.max(0d, Math.min(1d, closeLocation));
2181: 
2182:         double bearishBodyAtr = latest.getOpenPrice().subtract(latest.getClosePrice())
2183:                 .max(BigDecimal.ZERO).divide(atr, 12, RoundingMode.HALF_UP).doubleValue();
2184:         BigDecimal recentHigh = candles.stream()
2185:                 .map(Candle::getHighPrice)
2186:                 .filter(Objects::nonNull)
2187:                 .max(BigDecimal::compareTo)
2188:                 .orElse(latest.getHighPrice());
2189:         double recentHighFadeAtr = recentHigh.subtract(latest.getClosePrice())
2190:                 .max(BigDecimal.ZERO).divide(atr, 12, RoundingMode.HALF_UP).doubleValue();
2191: 
2192:         boolean closeBelowPrevious = latest.getClosePrice().compareTo(previous.getClosePrice()) < 0;
2193:         boolean bearishRejection = latest.getClosePrice().compareTo(latest.getOpenPrice()) < 0
2194:                 && closeBelowPrevious
2195:                 && bearishBodyAtr >= IMMEDIATE_PRICE_ACTION_MIN_BEARISH_BODY_ATR
2196:                 && closeLocation <= IMMEDIATE_PRICE_ACTION_MAX_CLOSE_LOCATION_FOR_BEARISH_REJECTION;
2197:         boolean exhaustedPop = closeBelowPrevious
2198:                 && recentHighFadeAtr >= IMMEDIATE_PRICE_ACTION_MIN_RECENT_HIGH_FADE_ATR
2199:                 && closeLocation <= IMMEDIATE_PRICE_ACTION_MAX_CLOSE_LOCATION_FOR_EXHAUSTED_POP;
2200: 
2201:         String summary = "closeLocation=" + format(closeLocation * 100d) + "%"
2202:                 + ", bearishBody=" + format(bearishBodyAtr) + " ATR"
2203:                 + ", fadeFromRecentHigh=" + format(recentHighFadeAtr) + " ATR"
2204:                 + ", closeVsPrevious=" + (closeBelowPrevious ? "LOWER" : "NOT_LOWER");
2205: 
2206:         if (bearishRejection || exhaustedPop) {
2207:             return ImmediatePriceAction.rejected(summary
2208:                     + (bearishRejection ? ", bearish rejection" : "")
2209:                     + (exhaustedPop ? ", exhausted-pop fade" : ""));
2210:         }
2211:         return ImmediatePriceAction.supportive(summary);
2212:     }

  35:  * AnalysisService remains the only producer of TradeSignal rows. This service never creates
